　动力律及类型：同 I-1 的程序律（Φ4a 门控积分、Φ5a 单阈、Φ1a ⊗g、Φ1b）；在 C\_LS⁺ 下历史动力只经阈门控、ρ = 0。族一ᵍ（阈值冲刷池网读法，模型假设）：汇点水池水位达临界即冲刷并排空（Φ4a ⊕ Φ5a），冲刷扩槽（Φ1a ⊗g），来水带松散物淤堵（Φ1b 的水力读法）。  
　承担历史动力的具体过程：自护持—枢轴：探针拍上阈门控枢轴使增强发生（该篇引理 14.5）。  
　证明或证据：（甲）模型逐项（该篇）；（乙）族一ᵍ 为模型假设，无物质认证；（丙）该篇在 C\_LS⁺ 下的条件结论（引用）。  
　区分协议：6.3（容与）与命题 4-1 适用于其两路读法的部分，未逐项迁移。未定项：第六节区分定理在 𝒦\_LS 上的迁移条件（U16-36）。

### 7.3　真实对象的条件应用（只到条件层；不含物质证据与职责的认证）

【C-1】果蝇蘑菇体（17 候选 04）  
　拟用模型：N5 型（Φ16）对 Huang 等回返多巴胺模型（方程未纳入对接）、TF3、RW、TWO。  
　条件预测：P-N5-1、P-N5-2、P-N5-4（6.4）。  
　未验前提：区室到层的对应；T 迷宫选择到首开读数的对应；测试本身的写入量；所据实验报告的事实待独立核实。  
　最小补充协议：短、长两臂非强化测试（定理 N5-4 的双臂协议）与短时单元的测后读出。  
【C-2】河床与水槽（17 候选 12）  
　拟用模型：族甲的河道读法（收支方向相反，待核）；N12 ≅ DAM 型（Φ13）；N13 ≅ KIR 型分汊（Φ15，Kirchhoff 分流为物理事实）；N15 型（Φ17，与决口扇、改道结构对应）。  
　条件预测：P-N12-1、P-N12-2；P-N13-1；P-N15-1。  
　未验前提：可阻断而维持来水；首开可读；满槽可判；新道的可读性。所据地貌学文献（Ganti 等、Chadwick 等的改道研究）只给改道现象，未给擦除或植入迹应的实验。  
【C-3】哺乳动物皮层与海马的比较与条件模型资料  
　拟用模型：N14（BIM）、IZD、TF3e（当拍门控）、c4 模式型。  
　条件预测：命题 N14-7（等待段）、命题 N14-8（共享态复位，带可分辨条件）、P-N14-1—P-N14-3。  
　未验前提：好恶输入的神经调质替身；无局部活动的等待可施；共用调质态可选择性复位而不触模式与深度。  
　定位：C-3 是哺乳动物（以啮齿类为主）的比较或条件模型资料，不承担人脑资格或人脑已用机制的认证；17 候选 05 人脑属未直接研究项（第十节）。  
【C-4】黏菌（17 候选 02）  
　拟用模型：族甲（恒压头）与 KIR（恒流量）；所据黏菌管网文献（Kramar 与 Alim 2021；Marbach 等 2023）。  
　条件预测：固定入口边界条件并同时测总通量或压头时，入口条件决定一管加深后另一管的绝对通量是否下降——恒压头（族甲）不降而总通量增，恒总流量（KIR 型，Φ15）下降而总通量不变；两者的份额变化相同，不作区分读数（7.2【I-2】）。  
　未验前提：入口边界条件的可控性；各管绝对通量与总通量（或压头）的可测性。

### 7.4　分不开的机制（等价范围、未知原因、最小补充协议）

　（一）N2 与 FS：W 实例层逐试次相等；文献层只定性对接。等价范围：W16-戊 全部协议。未知原因：所引文献无储量方程。最小补充：含储量方程的蓄满溢流文献。  
　（二）N3 与入口 LIF：W 实例层相等；试次内无漏失为近似。等价范围：W16-戊。最小补充：按 LIF 精确离散重写 N3 的试次内漏失。快漏盲区：ρ ≤ 1 − θ\_floor/θ\_min 的成员在一切 P\_lo \< θ\_floor 的恒压协议下与 C16 同为 ∅^n（定理 N3-6 的盲区）；最小补充协议未立（U16-12）。  
　（三）N5 与 Huang 等模型：该文献方程与 N5 的等价性未决；最小补充：该模型补充附录中的方程，及 P-N5-4 的短时单元读数。  
　（四）N13 与 KIR 原式、C16 ⊕ DN：三者都出现 S13；N13 与 KIR 原式不逐试次相等；N13 与 DN 的区分协议未立。  
　（五）N14 与宽读法 X3、IZHc：都出现 S14；N14 与 IZD 由协议 B 分开（命题 N14-7）；N14 与 IZHc 的分歧预测 P-N14-3 未立定理。  
　（六）N15 与 GWR、c4(GNG@到底)：都出现 S15；由溢量依赖分开（命题 N15-5）；对 v0.4-己 全类无不变量。  
　（七）N1b 与 C16 v0.2 在 Q₀ 下的对称盲区（命题 16-7，只读 Y）：扩大 W 加分路呈现后可分（命题 4-3）；单入口宿载上呈现等价于阻断。N1 全类在完整读数下的盲区另由定理 16-4 的首格胞构造给出，二者不互相顶替。  
　（八）族甲与 KIR 型的份额读数：单次状态的份额变化相同（例 J2a），以绝对通量与总量读数分开（7.2【I-2】）。  
　未知不读作负判。

八、误判界与盲区  
8.1　甲档：各区分定理在理想读数下零误判；成立条件是各定理题设中的网格裕度、窗口可达与参数盒。题设外不签。  
8.2　乙档（读数通道 R\_ε，定义 3-2）：统计量为申报尾段中报告为指定形态的次数 S，判则 S ≥ τ；一侧真值从不呈该形态，另一侧尾段恒呈该形态时，α ≤ P(Bin(n′, ε) ≥ τ)，β ≤ P(Bin(n′, 1 − ε) \< τ)（证明同定理 16-6）。显式数值（精确有理运算后转十进制，τ 取使二者较大者最小的值；附录 B.2 R6）：  
　ε = 1/20：n′ = 10，τ = 5，α ≤ 6.369e-05，β ≤ 2.755e-06；n′ = 20，τ = 10，α ≤ 1.134e-08，β ≤ 5.380e-10；n′ = 40，τ = 20，α ≤ 4.960e-16，β ≤ 2.474e-17。  
　ε = 1/10：n′ = 10，τ = 5，α ≤ 1.635e-03，β ≤ 1.469e-04；n′ = 20，τ = 10，α ≤ 7.151e-06，β ≤ 7.089e-07；n′ = 40，τ = 20，α ≤ 1.872e-10，β ≤ 1.960e-11。  
　ε = 1/5：n′ = 10，τ = 5，α ≤ 3.279e-02，β ≤ 6.369e-03；n′ = 20，τ = 10，α ≤ 2.595e-03，β ≤ 5.634e-04；n′ = 40，τ = 20，α ≤ 2.169e-05，β ≤ 5.027e-06。  
　应用：定理 N2-5（N2₌ 对 C16 v0.2，形态 = 两路同开，尾段 = 第 K 至 n 试次，n′ = n − K + 1）：ε = 1/10、n′ = 20、τ = 10 时 α ≤ 7.151e-06、β ≤ 7.089e-07。  
　定理 N3-7（N3 对定理 N3-7 所列比较子类，不含 STF；数值以 C16、N1、HOM 为例，协议 q\_N3 延长到 n = 8m，j\*\_max = 8，S = 报告非 ∅ 的试次数，α ≤ P(Bin(n, ε) ≥ τ)，β ≤ P(Bin(m, 1 − ε) \< τ)）：ε = 1/20 时 m = 10、20、40 分别为 α ≤ 4.659e-02、6.707e-03、1.810e-04，β ≤ 1.150e-02、2.574e-03、1.296e-04；ε = 1/40 时 m = 10、20、40 分别为 α ≤ 8.930e-04、1.376e-05、9.742e-10，β ≤ 1.643e-03、6.994e-06、2.818e-09（m = 20、n = 160 时 τ = 15）。ε 须小于约 1/(j\*\_max + 1) 才有正间隔。  
　定理 16-6（C16 v0.2 对 N1\[φ ≥ φ\_min\]）：α ≤ P(Bin(n, ε) ≥ τ)，β ≤ P(Bin(n − L, 1 − ε) \< τ)，数值按同一方法计算。  
　其余区分定理（6.4—6.9）只签甲档，乙档未立。  
8.3　通道类的量词：只凭上界 ε 不能保证对整个通道类统一的零误判（命题 16-6′）。  
8.4　离散化与网格：首开读数的一切区分都受网格 P/T 约束；连续阈差本身不保证读数差（命题 N13-2（三）、命题 N14-8（二）、命题 N15-2（二）（三））。凡定理题设含网格裕度者，裕度不成立时读数可同可不同，不签。  
8.5　盲区汇总：N1 全类在完整读数下没有统一的有限甲档协议，对每一有限协议都有首格胞构造的不可分成员（定理 16-4 盲区段）；N1b 对称成员在 Q₀ 下（命题 16-7）；N2 在独先路容量较大时的首开读数（定理 N2-4 的探针关闭）；N3 的快漏成员（定理 N3-6）；N5 与 Huang 等模型（方程未纳入对接）；N13 与 DNC；N14 与 X3、IZHc；N15 与 GWR、c4(GNG@到底) 之外的 v0.4-己 全类。  
8.6　有限核验：附录程序只覆盖其生成域，不是全组合穷尽；全称结论只来自证明。

九、适用域  
　（一）工作域：W16-戊 v0.1、W16-戊″、W16-己；有理参数、有限窗口、确定输入。命题 4-3 与 N13、N14、N15 所用的分路呈现只在有分路输入的宿载上是输入操作。  
　（二）比较类：各定理所指的版本（v0.1—v0.4、v0.3″、v0.4-己）与参数盒；等式对一切参数成立、成员资格只在盒内（D3.6）。  
　（三）分支：2.4 所列；常数退浅率前件（命题 N5-6）；K3 弃增前件（命题 4-1 的 I1）；h ≡ 0 前件（∅ 吸收）。  
　（四）实例：第七节逐行所列；他篇实例（s1、族甲、族戊、𝒦\_LS）上只用其已冻结的律与本篇的读法对应，职责判定以原篇为准。  
　（五）物质：一律条件应用。

十、17 候选去向索引  
本表只登本篇的研究去向，资格状态以《候选宿载场具化判定式》v1.0 第八节候选宿载总账为准。「未直接研究」不读作阴性。  
　01 大肠杆菌｜未直接研究｜—  
　02 多头绒泡菌｜条件应用 C-4｜族甲（I-2）、KIR（N13）  
　03 秀丽隐杆线虫｜未直接研究｜—  
　04 果蝇｜条件应用 C-1｜N5（I-6）  
　05 人脑｜未直接研究；C-3 为哺乳动物比较或条件模型资料，不承担人脑资格或已用机制的认证｜—  
　06 拟南芥｜未直接研究｜—  
　07 MLP｜未直接研究｜—  
　08 RNN/LSTM｜未直接研究｜—  
　09 Transformer｜未直接研究｜—  
　10 SNN｜模型实例 I-1、I-7｜s1（实例 P）、𝒦\_LS  
　11 储备池网络｜未直接研究｜—  
　12 河流沉积系统｜条件应用 C-2｜族甲河道读法、N12、N13、N15  
　13 反应—扩散系统｜未直接研究｜—  
　14 忆阻材料网络｜未直接研究｜—  
　15 颗粒物质｜未直接研究｜—  
　16 磁滞材料｜未直接研究｜—  
　17 引力环境 G17｜未直接研究｜—  
计数：直接研究或作条件应用者 4 项（02、04、10、12）；未直接研究者 13 项（01、03、05、06、07、08、09、11、13、14、15、16、17）。17 是类型计数，不是实例数。

十一、击穿方式（汇总）  
　核心击穿：第六节各定理的题设内出现与结论不符的运行（各条的击穿条件见各小节末）。  
　覆盖击穿：给出某候选与其构造覆盖成员（FS、LIF、IFBC、c1(C16, DAM)、c2(KIR, 1\[Y\])、c1(C16, IZH)、BIM 对称成员）在某协议上读数或状态不同的运行。  
　分类击穿：给出第七节某实例的律文本与所登动力类型不符（律中无该过程，或有未登的过程）。  
　读数击穿：在所报网格与窗口下，连续量的区分未转成有限读数的区分而被当作区分。  
　层级击穿：以模型结论顶替物质证据或职责判定；以「相对某冻结比较类不可表达」顶替「现实中独有」或「已发现新动力」。  
　版本击穿：比较所用的 W、C16、C\_cmp 版本与定理所指者不一致；K3、K7b、K11、K2 的分支取法改变而未重证所依的不变量。  
　不构成击穿：候选被覆盖（已报告）；盲区（已登记）；某对象无现成实验；未直接研究项。

十二、未决  
　U16-12　N3 快漏盲区的补充协议。  
　U16-13　Φ1b（MINUS）与间隔依赖退浅在 s1 上的区分协议：配对状态只改突触后迹的残余项比较、判定分辨率与误差界；I1′ 型不变量在 s1 上的数值核验。  
　U16-17　Huang 等果蝇模型的补充附录方程与 N5 的逐式比较。  
　U16-23　VST、BCM 与其连续时间原律的对接。  
　U16-24　DAM 与溃坝、冲刷文献方程的对接。  
　U16-27　KIR 在 ν ≠ 1 时与 Tero 等式 (4) 的对接。  
　U16-30　N15 相对 v0.4-己 全类的表达性。  
　U16-32　N14 的语义（动态、两阶段）非干涉版本。  
　U16-33　IZD 其他接口与离散化下命题 N14-7 是否保持；多档 P 首测（分开表现与写入）的协议。  
　U16-35　S13 与 I\_PD 型区分在绝对通量（或绝对电导）读数下的版本，须同时测总量并固定边界条件（族甲、族戊）；份额读数单独不区分恒压头与恒总流量。  
　U16-36　第六节区分定理在 𝒦\_LS 上的迁移条件。

十三、接口  
　向第十九篇《区室化第三因子回路迹应变换区分式》（果蝇蘑菇体回路）：定理 N5-4 的双臂协议与 C-1；命题 N5-6 的恢复判据。  
　向第二十一篇《地貌具化场律生成数理式》（河道地貌宿载）：C-2；定理 N12-1、N12-2、N12-4；定理 N13-3 的四臂诊断；定理 N15-3、命题 N15-5。  
　向第二十篇《人工神经网络迹应变换区分式》（脉冲神经网络宿载）：I-1 的 Φ1b 与 I1′；命题 4-1 与第 6.3 节在门控积分宿载上的改写条件。  
　向《物质场具化判定式》v1.0：乙档误判界在实测通道下的使用须按该篇的通道申报；R\_ε 只是模型层读数通道。  
　向《场具化职责对应数理式》v1.0：类型词表中同一代号的两实例之间的定理迁移，须按该篇的对应条件另证（5.1）。  
　主书形式律项的补全（K3 外沁、K7b 调谐控制写入、K11 容与之律、K2 的函数形式、着色、边沁的数量律）属主书的变更程序；本篇的候选闭合律只作为可供选择的形式读法与区分样本，不并入 K\_F。

## 附录 A　程序源码

运行环境：Python 3.10 以上，只用标准库与 fractions 的精确有理运算。以下 17 个文件置于同一目录：A.1—A.7 为本篇的库，A.8 与《候选宿载场具化判定式》v1.0 附录 A.1 逐字相同，A.9—A.17 为运行入口；运行入口只读同目录中附录 A 的文件（用于打印库文件的 SHA-256），不读其他文件。程序给出有限核验与精确见证；全称结论以正文的证明为准。  
A.1　t16b\_core.py　核心库：工作域 W16-戊 的试次、C16 v0.2 与 L₁ 组件（N1、STF、HOM、HS、HSsat、TF3、RW、TWO、FS、LIF）、N2、N3、N5 的前向关系与不变量核验函数

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""《沉积动力区分式》v1.0 附录核心库：工作域 W16-戊 与比较组件的前向关系。
只用标准库；一切数值为 fractions.Fraction。
一试次的输入：(P, u, Pres, r)
  P    峰值入口流压；入口流压按网格 p_t = h + t·P/T（t = 0..T）上升，h 为入口存量（无存量者 h ≡ 0）；
  u    强化输入 ∈ {0, 1}；Pres 呈现集 ⊆ {0, 1}（0 = a，1 = b）；r 试次后静置拍数。
开放：τ_x = min{t : x ∈ Pres 且 p_t ≥ θ_x}（无则 None）；Y = {x : τ_x = min τ}，全无则 Y = ∅。读数：逐试次 (Y, τ_min)。
成员（kind）：'C' 比较模型 C16；'N1' 疲劳；'N2' 满后守恒外沁；'FS' 蓄满溢流（独立实现）；'HS' 串扰；'HSsat' 夹限事件处切换到串扰；
  'N3' 入口积存释放；'LIF' 入口漏积分（独立实现）；'STF' 共用易化；'HOM' 静置驱动的内稳态；'TF3' 三因子；'RW'；'TWO' 双时程；'N5' 分层写入。
阈族：g(d) = g0/(1 + c·d)，0 < g0 ≤ 1，c > 0。
"""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import math
BOTH = frozenset((0, 1))

def F(v):
    return v if isinstance(v, Fr) else Fr(v)

def g(g0, c, d):
    return g0 / (1 + c * d)

def open_step(theta, h, P, T):
    """网格 p_t = h + t·P/T 上 p_t ≥ θ 的最小 t（0 ≤ t ≤ T）；无则 None。"""
    if h >= theta:
        return 0
    t = math.ceil((theta - h) * T / P)
    return t if t <= T else None

class M:
    """成员：kind 与参数字典 p；各参数按路为二元组者写作 (a, b)。"""
    def __init__(self, kind, **p):
        self.kind = kind
        self.p = {}
        for k, v in p.items():
            if isinstance(v, (tuple, list)):
                self.p[k] = tuple(None if x is None else F(x) for x in v)
            elif v is None:
                self.p[k] = None
            else:
                self.p[k] = F(v)
    def __getitem__(self, k):
        return self.p[k]
    def get(self, k, default=None):
        return self.p.get(k, default)
    def describe(self):
        items = []
        for k in sorted(self.p):
            v = self.p[k]
            if isinstance(v, tuple):
                items.append('%s=(%s)' % (k, ', '.join('∞' if x is None else str(x) for x in v)))
            else:
                items.append('%s=%s' % (k, v))
        return self.kind + ' ' + ' '.join(items)

def new_state(m, d=(0, 0), **kw):
    st = {'d': [F(d[0]), F(d[1])], 'h': F(kw.get('h', 0)),
          'f': [F(x) for x in kw.get('f', (0, 0))],
          'F': F(kw.get('Fac', 0)),
          'H': [F(x) for x in kw.get('H', (0, 0))],
          's': [F(x) for x in kw.get('s', (0, 0))],
          'L': [F(x) for x in kw.get('L', (0, 0))],
          'E': [F(x) for x in kw.get('E', (0, 0))],
          'df': [F(x) for x in kw.get('df', (0, 0))],
          'ds': [F(x) for x in kw.get('ds', (0, 0))]}
    if m.kind == 'TWO':
        st['d'] = [st['df'][0] + st['ds'][0], st['df'][1] + st['ds'][1]]
    return st

def copy_state(st):
    return {k: (list(v) if isinstance(v, list) else v) for k, v in st.items()}

def cap_of(m, x):
    c = m.get('cap')
    return None if c is None else c[x]

def clip(v, cap):
    return v if cap is None else min(v, cap)

def theta(m, st, x):
    k = m.kind
    g0, c = m['g0'][x], m['c'][x]
    if k in ('C', 'N2', 'HS', 'HSsat', 'N3', 'TF3', 'RW'):
        return g(g0, c, st['d'][x])
    if k == 'TWO':
        return g(g0, c, max(Fr(0), st['df'][x] + st['ds'][x]))
    if k == 'N1':
        return g(g0, c, st['d'][x]) + st['f'][x]
    if k == 'STF':
        return g(g0, c, st['d'][x]) * (1 - st['F'])
    if k == 'HOM':
        return g(g0, c, st['d'][x]) * (1 - st['H'][x])
    if k == 'N5':
        de = st['s'][x] + st['L'][x] - st['E'][x]
        return g(g0, c, de if de > 0 else Fr(0))
    if k == 'FS':
        return g(g0, c, st['d'][x])   # FS 另有独立实现 fs_trial；此处只供读阈
    if k == 'LIF':
        return g(g0, c, st['d'][x])
    raise ValueError(k)

def trial(m, st, T, P, u=0, pres=BOTH):
    """一次试次：返回 (Y, τ_min)，并就地按成员的律更新 st。"""
    P = F(P)
    if m.kind == 'FS':
        return fs_trial(m, st, T, P, pres)
    if m.kind == 'LIF':
        return lif_trial(m, st, T, P, pres)
    h = st['h'] if m.kind == 'N3' else Fr(0)
    taus = [open_step(theta(m, st, x), h, P, T) if x in pres else None for x in (0, 1)]
    fin = [t for t in taus if t is not None]
    if fin:
        tmin = min(fin)
        Y = frozenset(x for x in (0, 1) if taus[x] == tmin)
    else:
        tmin = None
        Y = frozenset()
    passage(m, st, Y, u, P, pres)
    return Y, tmin

def passage(m, st, Y, u, P, pres):
    k = m.kind
    if k in ('C', 'N1', 'STF', 'HOM', 'N3'):
        for x in Y:
            st['d'][x] = clip(st['d'][x] + m['alpha'][x], cap_of(m, x))
        if k == 'N1':
            for x in Y:
                st['f'][x] += m['phi']
                if m.get('fcap') is not None and st['f'][x] > m['fcap']:
                    st['f'][x] = m['fcap']
        if k == 'STF' and pres:
            st['F'] = st['F'] + m['kF'] * (m['Fbar'] - st['F'])
        if k == 'HOM':
            for x in Y:
                st['H'][x] = (1 - m['nu']) * st['H'][x]
        if k == 'N3':
            if Y:
                st['h'] = Fr(0)
            elif pres:
                st['h'] = m['rho'] * (st['h'] + P)
    elif k == 'N2':
        new = list(st['d'])
        exc = [Fr(0), Fr(0)]
        for x in Y:
            cp = cap_of(m, x)
            tot = st['d'][x] + m['alpha'][x]
            new[x] = clip(tot, cp)
            exc[x] = tot - new[x]
        for x in Y:
            o = 1 - x
            if exc[x] > 0:
                new[o] = clip(new[o] + exc[x], cap_of(m, o))
        st['d'] = new
    elif k == 'HS':
        new = list(st['d'])
        for x in Y:
            new[x] = clip(new[x] + m['alpha'][x], cap_of(m, x))
        for x in Y:
            o = 1 - x
            new[o] = clip(new[o] + m['kappa'], cap_of(m, o))
        st['d'] = new
    elif k == 'HSsat':
        new = list(st['d'])
        for x in Y:
            cp = cap_of(m, x)
            if cp is not None and st['d'][x] >= cp:
                o = 1 - x
                new[o] = clip(new[o] + m['alpha'][x], cap_of(m, o))
            else:
                new[x] = clip(new[x] + m['alpha'][x], cp)
        st['d'] = new
    elif k == 'TF3':
        if u:
            for x in Y:
                st['d'][x] = clip(st['d'][x] + m['alpha'][x], cap_of(m, x))
    elif k == 'RW':
        for x in Y:
            st['d'][x] = st['d'][x] + m['eta'] * (u * m['Vbar'] - st['d'][x])
    elif k == 'TWO':
        for x in Y:
            e = u * m['Vbar'] - (st['df'][x] + st['ds'][x])
            st['df'][x] += m['Bf'] * e
            st['ds'][x] += m['Bs'] * e
            st['d'][x] = st['df'][x] + st['ds'][x]
    elif k == 'N5':
        for x in Y:
            if u:
                st['s'][x] = min(st['s'][x] + m['alpha_s'], m['sbar'])
            elif st['s'][x] >= m['sigma']:
                st['L'][x] += m['beta']
            else:
                st['E'][x] += m['betaE']
    else:
        raise ValueError(k)

def rest(m, st, r):
    k = m.kind
    for _ in range(r):
        if k in ('C', 'N1', 'N2', 'HS', 'HSsat', 'N3', 'STF', 'HOM', 'TF3', 'RW', 'LIF'):
            for x in (0, 1):
                st['d'][x] = (1 - m['lam'][x]) * st['d'][x]
        if k == 'N1':
            for x in (0, 1):
                st['f'][x] = (1 - m['mu']) * st['f'][x]
        if k == 'N3':
            st['h'] = (1 - m['muh']) * st['h']
        if k == 'STF':
            st['F'] = (1 - m['muF']) * st['F']
        if k == 'HOM':
            for x in (0, 1):
                st['H'][x] = st['H'][x] + m['kH'] * (m['Hbar'] - st['H'][x])
        if k == 'TWO':
            for x in (0, 1):
                st['df'][x] = (1 - m['lf']) * st['df'][x]
                st['ds'][x] = (1 - m['ls']) * st['ds'][x]
                st['d'][x] = st['df'][x] + st['ds'][x]
        if k == 'N5':
            for x in (0, 1):
                st['s'][x] = (1 - m['mus']) * st['s'][x]
                st['E'][x] = (1 - m['muE']) * st['E'][x]
                st['L'][x] = (1 - m['muL']) * st['L'][x]
        if k == 'FS':
            for x in (0, 1):
                st['S'][x] = (1 - m['leak'][x]) * st['S'][x]
        if k == 'LIF':
            st['V'] = (1 - m['muV']) * st['V']

# ---------------- 独立实现：fill-and-spill（水文）与 LIF（神经） ----------------
def fs_state(S=(0, 0)):
    return {'S': [F(S[0]), F(S[1])]}

def fs_trial(m, st, T, P, pres):
    """fill-and-spill：两储水洼地 S_x（容量 Cap_x），入口来水 V_x 归首开者；洼地满则外溢入邻洼。
    首开竞争：阈 g(S_x)（储量愈大，出流门槛愈低）；网格同 W16-戊。"""
    taus = []
    for x in (0, 1):
        if x not in pres:
            taus.append(None)
            continue
        th = g(m['g0'][x], m['c'][x], st['S'][x])
        t = math.ceil(th * T / P)
        taus.append(t if t <= T else None)
    fin = [t for t in taus if t is not None]
    if not fin:
        return frozenset(), None
    tmin = min(fin)
    Y = frozenset(x for x in (0, 1) if taus[x] == tmin)
    fill = list(st['S'])
    spill = [Fr(0), Fr(0)]
    for x in Y:
        room = m['Cap'][x] - st['S'][x]
        take = m['V'][x] if m['V'][x] <= room else room
        fill[x] = st['S'][x] + take
        spill[x] = m['V'][x] - take
    for x in Y:
        o = 1 - x
        if spill[x] > 0:
            room = m['Cap'][o] - fill[o]
            fill[o] += spill[x] if spill[x] <= room else room
    st['S'] = fill
    return Y, tmin

def lif_state(V=0, d=(0, 0)):
    return {'V': F(V), 'd': [F(d[0]), F(d[1])]}

def lif_trial(m, st, T, P, pres):
    """入口为漏积分单元：膜电位 V 在试次内随输入线性上升 V + t·P/T，首达任一输出阈即放电（出口为首达者），
    放电后复位 V ← 0；未放电则试次末一步漏失 V ← ρ_V(V + P)。输出突触按 K1 加深（夹于容量）。"""
    taus = []
    for x in (0, 1):
        if x not in pres:
            taus.append(None)
            continue
        th = g(m['g0'][x], m['c'][x], st['d'][x])
        if st['V'] >= th:
            taus.append(0)
        else:
            t = math.ceil((th - st['V']) * T / P)
            taus.append(t if t <= T else None)
    fin = [t for t in taus if t is not None]
    if not fin:
        if pres:
            st['V'] = m['rhoV'] * (st['V'] + P)
        return frozenset(), None
    tmin = min(fin)
    Y = frozenset(x for x in (0, 1) if taus[x] == tmin)
    for x in Y:
        cp = m['cap'][x]
        st['d'][x] = st['d'][x] + m['alpha'][x] if cp is None else min(st['d'][x] + m['alpha'][x], cp)
    st['V'] = Fr(0)
    return Y, tmin

# ---------------- 运行 ----------------
def run(m, st0, T, trials):
    """trials = [(P, u, pres, r), ...]；返回 [(Y, τ_min), ...] 与末态。"""
    st = copy_state(st0)
    out = []
    for (P, u, pres, r) in trials:
        out.append(trial(m, st, T, P, u, pres))
        rest(m, st, r)
    return out, st

def tr(n, P=1, u=0, pres=BOTH, r=0):
    return [(F(P), u, frozenset(pres), r) for _ in range(n)]

def ystr(Y):
    if not Y:
        return '∅'
    return '{' + ','.join('ab'[x] for x in sorted(Y)) + '}'

def seqstr(out, with_tau=False):
    if with_tau:
        return ' '.join('%s@%s' % (ystr(Y), '-' if t is None else t) for (Y, t) in out)
    return ' '.join(ystr(Y) for (Y, _) in out)

# ---------------- 不变量（只读读数） ----------------
def viol_I1(out, trials):
    """I1：相邻两试次静置 0、P 相同、两路都呈现：前者独先 {x} ⇒ 后者 {x}。返回违反的 k 或 None。"""
    for k in range(len(out) - 1):
        P, u, pres, r = trials[k]
        P2, u2, pres2, r2 = trials[k + 1]
        if r != 0 or P != P2 or pres != BOTH or pres2 != BOTH:
            continue
        if len(out[k][0]) == 1 and out[k + 1][0] != out[k][0]:
            return k
    return None

def viol_I2(out, trials):
    """I2：P ≥ 1 且呈现集非空 ⇒ Y ≠ ∅。"""
    for k, ((Y, _), (P, u, pres, r)) in enumerate(zip(out, trials)):
        if P >= 1 and pres and not Y:
            return k
    return None

def viol_absorb(out, trials):
    """∅ 吸收：自某 ∅ 试次起，各试次 P 相同、两路都呈现（静置任意）时，其后又出现非 ∅。返回违反处 k 或 None。"""
    seen = None
    for k, ((Y, _), (P, u, pres, r)) in enumerate(zip(out, trials)):
        if pres != BOTH or (seen is not None and P != seen):
            seen = None
        if seen is not None and Y:
            return k
        if not Y and pres == BOTH:
            seen = P
    return None

def viol_I1p(out, trials, h0=0):
    """I1′：同 I1，另要求第 k 试次开始时入口存量 h_k = 0。h_k = 0 的充分来源：k 为首试次且初存量 h0 = 0，
    或前一试次非 ∅（开放清零，静置保持 0）。首对只在 h0 = 0 时纳入；h0 > 0 时首对不在申报域内，不计。"""
    for k in range(len(out) - 1):
        P, u, pres, r = trials[k]
        P2, u2, pres2, r2 = trials[k + 1]
        if r != 0 or P != P2 or pres != BOTH or pres2 != BOTH:
            continue
        if k == 0 and h0 != 0:
            continue
        if k > 0 and not out[k - 1][0]:
            continue
        if len(out[k][0]) == 1 and out[k + 1][0] != out[k][0]:
            return k
    return None

def viol_tau_nondecr(out, trials, x):
    """测试段（u = 0、静置 0、P 不变、两路呈现）中 x 独先时 τ 不降；返回违反处 k（τ 严格下降）或 None。"""
    prev = None
    for k, ((Y, t), (P, u, pres, r)) in enumerate(zip(out, trials)):
        if u != 0 or pres != BOTH:
            prev = None
            continue
        if Y == frozenset((x,)):
            if prev is not None and t < prev:
                return k
            prev = t
        if r != 0:
            prev = None
    return None

# ---------------- 精确二项尾 ----------------
def binom_tail_ge(n, p, k):
    p = F(p)
    q = 1 - p
    tot = Fr(0)
    for j in range(max(k, 0), n + 1):
        tot += math.comb(n, j) * p ** j * q ** (n - j)
    return tot

def binom_tail_lt(n, p, k):
    return 1 - binom_tail_ge(n, p, k)
~~~

A.2　t16c\_ext.py　扩展库：N11 与 IFBC、N12 与对照 C12、HOMB

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""《沉积动力区分式》v1.0 附录扩展库：N11（目标反馈）与积分反馈组件 IFBC 的独立实现；N12（阻断自身律）与对照 C12、HOMB。
只用标准库与 Fraction。
  N11：意向输入 z ∈ {0, 1, None}；每路 d（C16 的律）与努力 e ∈ [0, ē]；θ_x = max(0, g(d_x) − e_x)；
       试次后：z = x 时 Y = {x} 则 e_x ← max(0, e_x − κ_d)，否则 e_x ← min(ē, e_x + κ_u)；z ≠ x 时 e_x ← max(0, e_x − κ_d)；静置 d 退浅、e 不变。
  IFBC：同一律写成积分反馈 m ← clip(m + k_R(1 − a) − k_B·a, 0, m̄) 以 c1 减入 C16 之阈的独立实现。
  N12：阻断 Brk（协议置位）与坝后积头 H。试次开始：Brk_x = 1 且（H_x ≥ B̄_x 或本试次协议解除）则释放：Brk_x ← 0，d_x ← min(d_x + κ_s·H_x, D̄_x)，H_x ← 0；
       试次中 Brk_x = 1 之路不开放；试次后 Brk_x = 1 且 x 被呈现则 H_x ← H_x + ρ_H·P；静置 d 退浅、H ← (1 − μ_H)H。
  C12：同协议下 Brk 只由协议置解，无积头。HOMB：静置拍中被阻之路 H_x ← H_x + κ_H(H̄ − H_x)，阈乘 (1 − H_x)。
"""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import math
import t16b_core as tb
from t16b_core import g, open_step, BOTH
F = tb.F

# ---------------- N11 与 IFBC ----------------
def n11_new(d=(0, 0), e=(0, 0)):
    return {'d': [F(d[0]), F(d[1])], 'e': [F(e[0]), F(e[1])]}

def n11_theta(m, st, x):
    v = g(m['g0'][x], m['c'][x], st['d'][x]) - st['e'][x]
    return v if v > 0 else Fr(0)

def n11_trial(m, st, T, P, z, pres=BOTH):
    P = F(P)
    taus = [open_step(n11_theta(m, st, x), Fr(0), P, T) if x in pres else None for x in (0, 1)]
    fin = [t for t in taus if t is not None]
    Y = frozenset(x for x in (0, 1) if fin and taus[x] == min(fin)) if fin else frozenset()
    tmin = min(fin) if fin else None
    for x in Y:
        cp = m['cap'][x]
        st['d'][x] = st['d'][x] + m['alpha'][x] if cp is None else min(st['d'][x] + m['alpha'][x], cp)
    if m.kind == 'N11':
        for x in (0, 1):
            if z == x:
                if Y == frozenset((x,)):
                    st['e'][x] = max(Fr(0), st['e'][x] - m['kd'])
                else:
                    st['e'][x] = min(m['ebar'], st['e'][x] + m['ku'])
            else:
                st['e'][x] = max(Fr(0), st['e'][x] - m['kd'])
    elif m.kind == 'IFBC':
        # 积分反馈组件：每路一个甲基化样变量 mm_x（存于 st['e']），活性 a_x = 1 当且仅当「x 非目标」或「x 为目标且独先达成」；
        # 离散化 mm ← clip(mm + k_R(1 − a) − k_B·a, 0, m̄)，k_R = κ_u，k_B = κ_d，m̄ = ē；c2：阈偏移 = mm。
        for x in (0, 1):
            a = 0 if (z == x and Y != frozenset((x,))) else 1
            v = st['e'][x] + m['ku'] * (1 - a) - m['kd'] * a
            st['e'][x] = min(m['ebar'], max(Fr(0), v))
    return Y, tmin

def n11_rest(m, st, r):
    for _ in range(r):
        for x in (0, 1):
            st['d'][x] = (1 - m['lam'][x]) * st['d'][x]

def n11_run(m, st0, T, trials):
    st = {'d': list(st0['d']), 'e': list(st0['e'])}
    out = []
    for (P, z, pres, r) in trials:
        out.append(n11_trial(m, st, T, P, z, pres))
        n11_rest(m, st, r)
    return out, st

# ---------------- N12、C12、HOMB ----------------
def n12_new(d=(0, 0)):
    return {'d': [F(d[0]), F(d[1])], 'H': [Fr(0), Fr(0)], 'brk': [0, 0], 'Hm': [Fr(0), Fr(0)], 'events': []}

def n12_theta(m, st, x):
    th = g(m['g0'][x], m['c'][x], st['d'][x])
    if m.kind == 'HOMB':
        th = th * (1 - st['Hm'][x])
    return th

def n12_trial(m, st, T, P, pres=BOTH, block=(), unblock=(), k=0):
    P = F(P)
    for x in block:
        st['brk'][x] = 1
    for x in (0, 1):
        if st['brk'][x] == 1:
            if m.kind == 'N12' and (st['H'][x] >= m['Bbar'][x] or x in unblock):
                st['brk'][x] = 0
                cp = m['cap'][x]
                nd = st['d'][x] + m['ks'] * st['H'][x]
                st['d'][x] = nd if cp is None else min(nd, cp)
                st['events'].append((k, 'breach' if x not in unblock else 'release', x, st['H'][x]))
                st['H'][x] = Fr(0)
            elif x in unblock:
                st['brk'][x] = 0
    taus = []
    for x in (0, 1):
        if x in pres and st['brk'][x] == 0:
            taus.append(open_step(n12_theta(m, st, x), Fr(0), P, T))
        else:
            taus.append(None)
    fin = [t for t in taus if t is not None]
    Y = frozenset(x for x in (0, 1) if fin and taus[x] == min(fin)) if fin else frozenset()
    tmin = min(fin) if fin else None
    for x in Y:
        cp = m['cap'][x]
        st['d'][x] = st['d'][x] + m['alpha'][x] if cp is None else min(st['d'][x] + m['alpha'][x], cp)
        if m.kind == 'HOMB':
            st['Hm'][x] = (1 - m['nu']) * st['Hm'][x]
    if m.kind == 'N12':
        for x in (0, 1):
            if st['brk'][x] == 1 and x in pres:
                st['H'][x] += m['rhoH'] * P
    return Y, tmin

def n12_rest(m, st, r):
    for _ in range(r):
        for x in (0, 1):
            st['d'][x] = (1 - m['lam'][x]) * st['d'][x]
            if m.kind == 'N12':
                st['H'][x] = (1 - m['muH']) * st['H'][x]
            if m.kind == 'HOMB' and st['brk'][x] == 1:
                st['Hm'][x] = st['Hm'][x] + m['kH'] * (m['Hbar'] - st['Hm'][x])

def n12_run(m, st0, T, trials):
    """trials = [(P, pres, r, block, unblock), ...]"""
    st = {'d': list(st0['d']), 'H': list(st0['H']), 'brk': list(st0['brk']), 'Hm': list(st0['Hm']), 'events': []}
    out = []
    for k, (P, pres, r, block, unblock) in enumerate(trials):
        out.append(n12_trial(m, st, T, P, pres, block, unblock, k + 1))
        n12_rest(m, st, r)
    return out, st
~~~

A.3　t16d\_ext.py　扩展库：带阻断的通用试次（L₁）、组合 HSP、N5 恢复的精确工具、N12 破塞时刻

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""《沉积动力区分式》v1.0 附录扩展库：带阻断的通用试次（比较组件库 L₁）、组合 HSP、N5 恢复的精确工具、N12 破塞时刻。
只用标准库与 Fraction。
  trial_blk：阻断之路不开放；呈现集照样传给通过后更新。
  HSP：串扰增量以被串入之路的呈现 1[o ∈ Pres] 门控（c2，严读法合法）。
  eventual_sign、delta_R、unique_exact：N5 恢复的终局符号、有效深与精确首开步比较；breach_trial：含初始积头的破塞时刻。
"""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import math
import t16b_core as tb
import t16c_ext as tc
from t16b_core import g, open_step, BOTH
F = tb.F

def trial_blk(m, st, T, P, u=0, pres=BOTH, blocked=frozenset()):
    P = F(P)
    eff = frozenset(x for x in pres if x not in blocked)
    if m.kind == 'FS':
        return tb.fs_trial(m, st, T, P, eff)
    if m.kind == 'LIF':
        return tb.lif_trial(m, st, T, P, eff) if eff else _lif_noeff(m, st, P, pres)
    if m.kind == 'HSP':
        taus = [open_step(tb.g(m['g0'][x], m['c'][x], st['d'][x]), Fr(0), P, T) if x in eff else None for x in (0, 1)]
        fin = [t for t in taus if t is not None]
        Y = frozenset(x for x in (0, 1) if fin and taus[x] == min(fin)) if fin else frozenset()
        new = list(st['d'])
        for x in Y:
            new[x] = tb.clip(new[x] + m['alpha'][x], tb.cap_of(m, x))
        for x in Y:
            o = 1 - x
            if o in pres:                      # c2：增量 κ 乘以门控 1[o ∈ Pres]
                new[o] = tb.clip(new[o] + m['kappa'], tb.cap_of(m, o))
        st['d'] = new
        return Y, (min(fin) if fin else None)
    h = st['h'] if m.kind == 'N3' else Fr(0)
    taus = [open_step(tb.theta(m, st, x), h, P, T) if x in eff else None for x in (0, 1)]
    fin = [t for t in taus if t is not None]
    Y = frozenset(x for x in (0, 1) if fin and taus[x] == min(fin)) if fin else frozenset()
    tb.passage(m, st, Y, u, P, pres)
    return Y, (min(fin) if fin else None)

def _lif_noeff(m, st, P, pres):
    if pres:
        st['V'] = m['rhoV'] * (st['V'] + P)
    return frozenset(), None

def rest_any(m, st, r):
    if m.kind == 'HSP':
        for _ in range(r):
            for x in (0, 1):
                st['d'][x] = (1 - m['lam'][x]) * st['d'][x]
    else:
        tb.rest(m, st, r)

def run_blk(m, st0, T, trials):
    """trials = [(P, u, pres, r, blocked), ...]"""
    st = tb.copy_state(st0)
    out = []
    for (P, u, pres, r, blk) in trials:
        out.append(trial_blk(m, st, T, P, u, pres, blk))
        rest_any(m, st, r)
    return out, st

# ---------------- N5 恢复的精确工具 ----------------
def eventual_sign(s0, L0, E0, mus, muL, muE):
    """δ_R 未截断值 s0·ρs^R + L0·ρL^R − E0·ρE^R（ρ = 1 − μ）的终局符号：按衰减率合并系数，取最大衰减率（最慢衰减）上的非零合并系数之号。
    返回 +1、−1 或 0（全部合并系数为 0）。ρ = 0 的项只在 R = 0 起作用，不入终局。"""
    terms = {}
    for coef, mu in ((F(s0), F(mus)), (F(L0), F(muL)), (-F(E0), F(muE))):
        r = 1 - mu
        if coef != 0 and r > 0:
            terms[r] = terms.get(r, Fr(0)) + coef
    for r in sorted(terms, reverse=True):
        if terms[r] != 0:
            return 1 if terms[r] > 0 else -1
    return 0

def delta_R(s0, L0, E0, mus, muL, muE, R):
    v = F(s0) * (1 - F(mus)) ** R + F(L0) * (1 - F(muL)) ** R - F(E0) * (1 - F(muE)) ** R
    return v if v > 0 else Fr(0)

def unique_exact(g0, c, delta, P, T):
    """两路同阈机制、b 路有效深 0：两路首开步 ⌈θ·T/P⌉（θ ≤ g0 ≤ 1 ≤ P 时都可达）比较；返回 (τ_a, τ_b, a 独先?)"""
    ta = math.ceil(F(g0) / (1 + F(c) * delta) * T / F(P))
    tbb = math.ceil(F(g0) * T / F(P))
    return ta, tbb, ta < tbb

# ---------------- N12 破塞时刻 ----------------
def breach_trial(Bbar, H0, rhoH, P):
    """静置 0、第 1 试次一次性置位阻断、此后每试次被阻之路被呈现、P 恒定、ρ_H·P > 0：破塞所在试次（1 起计）。"""
    x = F(Bbar) - F(H0)
    if x <= 0:
        return 1
    return 1 + math.ceil(x / (F(rhoH) * F(P)))
~~~

A.4　t16e\_ext.py　扩展库：C\_cmp v0.2 的新增组件（VST、BCM、CMP、DAM、PF、ERO、DIS、THR）与 N13

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""《沉积动力区分式》v1.0 附录扩展库：比较类 C_cmp v0.2 的新增组件与 N13。
只用标准库与 Fraction。
统一状态：d、brk（阻断）、H（积存）、cnt（被阻且被呈现的计数）、ub（低通驱动）、thM（BCM 滑动阈）。
统一试次（执行合同 D2.4）：
  ①′ 协议置位或解除阻断；自解组件检查条件（PF：H ≥ B̄；ERO：cnt ≥ K_b；DIS：本试次 u = 1；THR：本试次 P ≥ P_rel；DAM：同 PF 并冲刷）；
  ①② 只对呈现且未阻断之路算阈与全局首开集合 Y；
  ③ K1/K3 → CMP（未通过之路减 κ_c）→ VST（呈现之路按驱动强度写入）→ BCM → PF/DAM/ERO 积存；
  ④ 静置：K2；H ← (1 − μ_H)H；ub ← (1 − μ_u)ub；thM ← (1 − μ_M)thM。
组件开关由参数是否给出决定；kind 只作标签。
"""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import math
import t16b_core as tb
from t16b_core import g, open_step, BOTH
F = tb.F

def new(d=(0, 0), H=(0, 0)):
    return {'d': [F(d[0]), F(d[1])], 'brk': [0, 0], 'H': [F(H[0]), F(H[1])], 'cnt': [0, 0],
            'ub': [Fr(0), Fr(0)], 'thM': [Fr(0), Fr(0)], 'events': []}

def cp(st):
    return {k: (list(v) if isinstance(v, list) else v) for k, v in st.items()}

def has(m, k):
    return m.get(k) is not None

def trial(m, st, T, P, u=0, pres=BOTH, block=(), unblock=(), k=0):
    P = F(P)
    # ① 阻断置解与自解
    for x in block:
        st['brk'][x] = 1
    for x in (0, 1):
        if st['brk'][x] != 1:
            continue
        release = x in unblock
        why = 'release' if release else None
        if not release and has(m, 'Bbar') and st['H'][x] >= m['Bbar'][x]:
            release, why = True, 'pressure'
        if not release and has(m, 'Kb') and st['cnt'][x] >= m['Kb']:
            release, why = True, 'erosion'
        if not release and has(m, 'dis') and u == 1:
            release, why = True, 'disinhibition'
        if not release and has(m, 'Prel') and P >= m['Prel']:   # THR：本试次驱动超阈即释放（过载型）
            release, why = True, 'overload'
        if release:
            st['brk'][x] = 0
            if has(m, 'ks'):                       # DAM：释放时按积头冲刷
                nd = st['d'][x] + m['ks'] * st['H'][x]
                cpx = tb.cap_of(m, x)
                st['d'][x] = nd if cpx is None else min(nd, cpx)
            st['events'].append((k, why, x))
            st['H'][x] = Fr(0); st['cnt'][x] = 0
    # ② 全局首开集合
    taus = []
    for x in (0, 1):
        if x in pres and st['brk'][x] == 0:
            taus.append(open_step(g(m['g0'][x], m['c'][x], st['d'][x]), Fr(0), P, T))
        else:
            taus.append(None)
    fin = [t for t in taus if t is not None]
    Y = frozenset(x for x in (0, 1) if fin and taus[x] == min(fin)) if fin else frozenset()
    # ③ 通过后更新（门控读试次前状态：此处各组件只读本试次的 Y、P、Pres、Brk）
    pre_brk = list(st['brk'])
    for x in Y:
        st['d'][x] = tb.clip(st['d'][x] + m['alpha'][x], tb.cap_of(m, x))
    if has(m, 'kc'):                               # CMP：异突触资源竞争（N-13 Triesch 型）在 W 上的实例：x 通过时未通过之路减 κ_c
        for x in Y:
            o = 1 - x
            if o not in Y:
                st['d'][o] = max(Fr(0), st['d'][o] - m['kc'])
    if has(m, 'Ap'):                               # VST：电压型（Clopath 型）在 W 上的实例
        for x in (0, 1):
            if x in pres:
                gate = 1 if not has(m, 'vst_blk_only') else pre_brk[x]
                st['ub'][x] = (1 - m['muu']) * st['ub'][x] + m['muu'] * P
                ltp = m['Ap'] * max(Fr(0), P - m['thp']) * max(Fr(0), st['ub'][x] - m['thm'])
                st['d'][x] = tb.clip(st['d'][x] + gate * ltp, tb.cap_of(m, x))
    if has(m, 'eta'):                              # BCM：y = P·1[x ∈ Y]
        for x in (0, 1):
            y = P if x in Y else Fr(0)
            dd = m['eta'] * y * (y - st['thM'][x])
            st['d'][x] = tb.clip(max(Fr(0), st['d'][x] + dd), tb.cap_of(m, x))
            st['thM'][x] = (1 - m['muM']) * st['thM'][x] + m['muM'] * y * y
    for x in (0, 1):
        if st['brk'][x] == 1 and x in pres:
            if has(m, 'rhoH'):
                st['H'][x] += m['rhoH'] * P
            st['cnt'][x] += 1
    return Y, (min(fin) if fin else None)

def rest(m, st, r):
    for _ in range(r):
        for x in (0, 1):
            st['d'][x] = (1 - m['lam'][x]) * st['d'][x]
            if has(m, 'muH'):
                st['H'][x] = (1 - m['muH']) * st['H'][x]
            if has(m, 'muu'):
                st['ub'][x] = (1 - m['muu']) * st['ub'][x]
            if has(m, 'muM'):
                st['thM'][x] = (1 - m['muM']) * st['thM'][x]

def run(m, st0, T, trials):
    """trials = [(P, u, pres, r, block, unblock), ...]"""
    st = cp(st0)
    out = []
    for k, (P, u, pres, r, b, ub) in enumerate(trials):
        out.append(trial(m, st, T, P, u, pres, b, ub, k + 1))
        rest(m, st, r)
    return out, st

# ---------------- N13（分流与按份额写入） ----------------
def n13_run(m, d0, T, trials):
    """两路共享入口流压 p_t = t·P/T；呈现之路分得分压 w_x·p_t，w_x = (1 + d_x)^ν / Σ_{y 呈现} (1 + d_y)^ν（形变势之分布，ν 取正整数）；
    x 开放当 w_x·p_t ≥ θ₀（θ₀ 两路同，不随深度变：深度只经分压起作用）；通过后 d_x += α·w_x（势构形：冲深随所受分压）；静置 d ← (1 − λ)d。
    trials = [(P, pres, r), ...]"""
    d = [F(d0[0]), F(d0[1])]
    out = []
    for (P, pres, r) in trials:
        P = F(P)
        ws = {x: (1 + d[x]) ** m['nu'] for x in pres}
        tot = sum(ws.values())
        w = {x: ws[x] / tot for x in pres}
        taus = []
        for x in (0, 1):
            if x in pres:
                t = math.ceil(m['th0'] * T / (w[x] * P))
                taus.append(t if t <= T else None)
            else:
                taus.append(None)
        fin = [t for t in taus if t is not None]
        Y = frozenset(x for x in (0, 1) if fin and taus[x] == min(fin)) if fin else frozenset()
        for x in Y:
            d[x] += m['alpha'] * w[x]
        for _ in range(r):
            d = [(1 - m['lam']) * v for v in d]
        out.append((Y, min(fin) if fin else None))
    return out, d
~~~

A.5　t16f\_ext.py　扩展库：C\_cmp v0.3 的 KIR、DNC 与 W16-戊″ 上的 N14 及其比较成员

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""《沉积动力区分式》v1.0 附录扩展库：比较类 C_cmp v0.3 的 KIR、DNC 与 W16-戊″ 上的 N14 及其比较成员。
只用标准库与 Fraction。
一、KIR（并联分流强化）：池 = 本试次呈现且未阻断之路；D_x = (1 + d_x)^ν；s_x = D_x/Σ_池 D_y；
  x 在首个 s_x·p_t ≥ θ₀ 的网格步开放，τ_x = ⌈θ₀·T/(s_x·P)⌉（≤ T 时）；gateY = 0 时池中每路 d_x += α·s_x^μ，gateY = 1 时只对 x ∈ Y；静置 d ← (1 − λ)d。
二、DNC（C16 加除法归一化阈项）：R_x = D_x^n/(σ^n + Σ_池 D_y^n)，D_x = 1 + d_x；θ_x = g(d_x) + θ_N·(1/R_x − 1)；通过按 K1；静置 K2。
三、N14 与 q14 的比较成员（情感输入 e ∈ {−1, 0, +1}）。
"""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import math
import t16b_core as tb
from t16b_core import g, open_step, BOTH
F = tb.F

def _argmin(taus):
    fin = [t for t in taus if t is not None]
    if not fin:
        return frozenset(), None
    t = min(fin)
    return frozenset(x for x in (0, 1) if taus[x] == t), t

# ---------------- KIR ----------------
def kir_run(m, d0, T, trials, gateY=1):
    """m: nu, th0, alpha, mu（正整数）, lam；trials = [(P, pres, r, block), ...]（block 为本试次阻断之路的集合）"""
    d = [F(d0[0]), F(d0[1])]
    out = []
    for (P, pres, r, blk) in trials:
        P = F(P)
        pool = [x for x in (0, 1) if x in pres and x not in blk]
        s = {}
        if pool:
            D = {x: (1 + d[x]) ** int(m['nu']) for x in pool}
            tot = sum(D.values())
            s = {x: D[x] / tot for x in pool}
        taus = []
        for x in (0, 1):
            if x in s:
                t = math.ceil(m['th0'] * T / (s[x] * P))
                taus.append(t if t <= T else None)
            else:
                taus.append(None)
        Y, tm = _argmin(taus)
        for x in pool:
            if (not gateY) or (x in Y):
                d[x] = d[x] + m['alpha'] * s[x] ** int(m['mu'])
        for _ in range(r):
            d = [(1 - m['lam']) * v for v in d]
        out.append((Y, tm))
    return out, d

# ---------------- DNC ----------------
def dnc_run(m, d0, T, trials):
    """c1(C16 v0.2, DN)：m: g0, c, alpha, lam（二元组）, n, sigma, thN；trials = [(P, pres, r), ...]"""
    d = [F(d0[0]), F(d0[1])]
    out = []
    for (P, pres, r) in trials:
        P = F(P)
        pool = [x for x in (0, 1) if x in pres]
        n = int(m['n'])
        tot = m['sigma'] ** n + sum((1 + d[y]) ** n for y in pool)
        taus = []
        for x in (0, 1):
            if x in pool:
                R = (1 + d[x]) ** n / tot
                th = g(m['g0'][x], m['c'][x], d[x]) + m['thN'] * (1 / R - 1)
                taus.append(open_step(th, Fr(0), P, T))
            else:
                taus.append(None)
        Y, tm = _argmin(taus)
        for x in Y:
            d[x] = d[x] + m['alpha'][x]
        for _ in range(r):
            d = [(1 - m['lam'][x]) * d[x] for x in (0, 1)]
        out.append((Y, tm))
    return out, d

# ---------------- N14 与 q14 的比较成员 ----------------
def clip(v, lo, hi):
    return lo if v < lo else (hi if v > hi else v)

def e_run(m, st0, T, trials):
    """统一实现 N14 与比较成员。状态：d（两路深度）、c（附于意象的着色，两路共用）、F（共用易化）、V（每路值）。
    trials = [(P, pres, r, e), ...]。组件开关由参数是否给出决定：
      核：θ_x = g(d_x)·(1 − F)（无 STF 时 F ≡ 0）；通过 d_x += α·因子（下列）后夹于 [0, D̄]；静置 d ← (1 − λ)d。
      N14：因子 = 1 + κ·c；有 Y 的试次后 c ← clip(c + β·e, −c̄, c̄)；静置 c ← (1 − μ_c)c。
      TF3e：通过时另加 α_e·e（带符号三因子，只写通过之路）。
      RWe：通过时 d_x ← d_x + η(e·V̄ − d_x)（带符号 RW，只写通过之路）。
      HS：x 通过时另一路 += κ_h；HSg：同上但乘 1[e = +1]（严读法 c2，以 e 门控跨路写入）。
      STF：有呈现的试次后 F ← F + κ_F(F̄ − F)；STFg：κ_F 乘 1[e = +1]（严读法 c2，以 e 门控共用状态）。
      X3：每路值 V_x 在通过时 V_x ← V_x + η_v(e − V_x)；任一路 y 的 K1 增量乘 clip(a₀ + b₁·V_{另一路}, 0, 1)（宽读法 c2，以他路状态门控）。
    """
    st = {'d': [F(st0['d'][0]), F(st0['d'][1])], 'c': F(st0.get('c', 0)), 'F': Fr(0), 'V': [Fr(0), Fr(0)]}
    out = []
    has = lambda k: m.get(k) is not None
    for (P, pres, r, e) in trials:
        P = F(P)
        taus = []
        for x in (0, 1):
            if x in pres:
                taus.append(open_step(g(m['g0'], m['cc'], st['d'][x]) * (1 - st['F']), Fr(0), P, T))
            else:
                taus.append(None)
        Y, tm = _argmin(taus)
        new = list(st['d'])
        for x in Y:
            fac = Fr(1)
            if has('kappa'):
                fac = 1 + m['kappa'] * st['c']
            if has('a0'):
                fac = fac * clip(m['a0'] + m['b1'] * st['V'][1 - x], Fr(0), Fr(1))
            v = new[x] + m['alpha'] * fac
            if has('alpha_e'):
                v += m['alpha_e'] * e
            if has('eta'):
                v = v + m['eta'] * (e * m['Vbar'] - v)
            new[x] = v
        if has('kh'):
            for x in Y:
                if (1 - x) not in Y:
                    gate = 1 if not has('hs_gate') else (1 if e == 1 else 0)
                    new[1 - x] = new[1 - x] + gate * m['kh']
        cap = m.get('cap')
        st['d'] = [clip(v, Fr(0), v if cap is None else cap) for v in new]
        if has('a0'):
            for x in Y:
                st['V'][x] = st['V'][x] + m['eta_v'] * (e - st['V'][x])
        if has('kF') and pres:
            gate = 1 if not has('stf_gate') else (1 if e == 1 else 0)
            st['F'] = st['F'] + gate * m['kF'] * (m['Fbar'] - st['F'])
        if has('kappa') and Y:
            st['c'] = clip(st['c'] + m['beta'] * e, -m['cbar'], m['cbar'])
        for _ in range(r):
            st['d'] = [(1 - m['lam']) * v for v in st['d']]
            if has('kappa'):
                st['c'] = (1 - m['muc']) * st['c']
            if has('kF'):
                st['F'] = (1 - m['muF']) * st['F']
        out.append((Y, tm))
    return out, st
~~~

A.6　t16g\_ext.py　扩展库：c4 夹限切换对照、C\_cmp v0.4 的 IZH、IZHc、TON、PIT，W16-己 上的 N15 与 GNG、GWR、MSP

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""《沉积动力区分式》v1.0 附录扩展库：c4 夹限切换的对照模型、C_cmp v0.4 的调质与价值组件、W16-己 上的 N15 与生成组件。
只用标准库与 Fraction。
一、c4_example：a 的夹限事件经 c4 切换 b 的写入律（非干涉类之外的对照）。
二、m_run（W16-戊″）：IZH（共用调质水平 D，有开放的试次后 D ← clip(D + ε_D·e, −D̄, D̄)；即时资格或跨试次资格迹；写入 α_I·c_x·D）；
  IZHc（资格迹、逐试次衰减）；TON（共用平均奖赏 R，作用于阈）；PIT（共用意象价值 V，作用于阈）。
三、s_run（W16-己：路 a、b 与 a 边的潜在结构位 ℓ）：N15、GNG、c4(GNG@到底)、GWR、MSP。
"""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import math
import t16b_core as tb
from t16b_core import g, open_step
F = tb.F

def clip(v, lo, hi):
    return lo if v < lo else (hi if v > hi else v)

def _argmin(taus, keys):
    fin = [taus[k] for k in keys if taus[k] is not None]
    if not fin:
        return frozenset(), None
    t = min(fin)
    return frozenset(k for k in keys if taus[k] == t), t

# ---------------- 一、c4 夹限切换对照 ----------------
def c4_example(e, T=100, n=2):
    """预训练 1 次只呈现 a（P = 1，g0 = 1）：a 的 TF3e 增量 e，容量 1/2；c4 以 a 的「到底」事件把 b 的写入律由 α_b = 0 切为 α_b = 1/4。
    其后只测 b n 次（e = 0）。返回（预训练 Y，b 的首开步列表）。"""
    da, db, mode = Fr(0), Fr(0), False
    th_a = g(1, 1, da)
    Ypre = frozenset((0,)) if open_step(th_a, Fr(0), Fr(1), T) is not None else frozenset()
    raw = da + e
    da = min(Fr(1, 2), max(Fr(0), raw))
    if raw > Fr(1, 2):
        mode = True                      # a 的夹限事件（受 e 影响）
    out = []
    for _ in range(n):
        out.append(open_step(g(1, 1, db), Fr(0), Fr(1), T))
        if mode:
            db += Fr(1, 4)
    return Ypre, out

# ---------------- 二、调质与价值组件 ----------------
def m_run(m, d0, T, trials):
    """trials = [(P, pres, r, e), ...]"""
    has = lambda k: m.get(k) is not None
    d = [F(d0[0]), F(d0[1])]
    D = Fr(0); R = Fr(0); V = Fr(0); el = [Fr(0), Fr(0)]
    out = []
    for (P, pres, r, e) in trials:
        P = F(P)
        fac = Fr(1)
        if has('psiT'):
            fac -= m['psiT'] * max(Fr(0), R)
        if has('psiV'):
            fac -= m['psiV'] * V
        fac = clip(fac, Fr(0), Fr(1))
        taus = {x: (open_step(g(m['g0'], m['cc'], d[x]) * fac, Fr(0), P, T) if x in pres else None) for x in (0, 1)}
        Y, tm = _argmin(taus, (0, 1))
        new = list(d)
        for x in Y:
            new[x] = new[x] + m['alpha']
        if has('alphaI'):
            for x in (0, 1):
                if has('mu_el'):
                    el[x] = (1 - m['mu_el']) * el[x] + (1 if x in Y else 0)
                else:
                    el[x] = Fr(1) if x in Y else Fr(0)
                new[x] = new[x] + m['alphaI'] * el[x] * D
        d = [max(Fr(0), v) if m.get('cap') is None else clip(v, Fr(0), m['cap']) for v in new]
        if Y:
            if has('alphaI'):
                D = clip(D + m['epsD'] * e, -m['Dbar'], m['Dbar'])
            if has('etaR'):
                R = R + m['etaR'] * (max(0, e) - R)
            if has('etaV'):
                V = V + m['etaV'] * (e - V)
        if has('alphaI') and has('da_per_trial'):
            D = (1 - m['muD']) * D
        for _ in range(r):
            d = [(1 - m['lam']) * v for v in d]
            if has('alphaI'):
                D = (1 - m['muD']) * D
        out.append((Y, tm))
    return out, {'d': d, 'D': D, 'R': R, 'V': V}

# ---------------- 三、W16-己 与 N15 ----------------
L = 2   # 潜在结构位 ℓ 的编号

def s_run(m, st0, T, trials):
    """W16-己：路 a(0)、b(1)；ℓ(2) 为 a 边上的潜在结构位，生成前不存在；生成后为一路，呈现当且仅当 a 被呈现（边沁之息与域核之息近相关），
    有自己的阻断标志（阻断 a 不阻断 ℓ）。trials = [(P, pres, r, block)]，block 为本试次阻断之路的集合（只就本试次）。
    生成律（由参数决定）：
      N15：a 通过且通过前已满（d_a = D̄_a）时，增量 α_a 入 ℓ 的生成量 s；s ≥ σ 时生成，d_ℓ = s − σ，s ← 0；静置 s ← (1 − μ_s)s。
      GNG（Fritzke 1995 的 W 读法）：每路误差 err_x 在 x 通过时增 θ_x²（试次前之阈，读作量化误差）；每 λ 个有呈现的试次，若误差最大者为 a 且 ℓ 未生成，
        则在 a 与其邻 b 之间插入 ℓ：d_ℓ = (d_a + d_b)/2，err_a、err_b 乘 α_G，err_ℓ = 新 err_a；每试次末全部误差乘 d_G。
        gng_after_full 给出时为 c4 组合：插入律只在 a 的「到底」事件发生之后启用，且计数自该事件起算。
      GWR（Marsland、Shapiro 与 Nehmzow 2002 的 W 读法）：a 的发放计数 h（初值 1）在 a 通过时按 Stanley 习惯化的一步离散 h ← max(0, h + (α_h(1 − h) − 1)/τ_h)；
        失配读作「通过前已满仍入」；失配且 h < h_T 时在 a 边插入 ℓ，d_ℓ = d_a/2（文献式 w_r = (w_s + x)/2 的 W 读法，x 读作 0）。
      MSP（Butz–van Ooyen 2013 的 W 读法，线性生长曲线按通行写法，与原文方程的逐式对接未建立）：a 的活动 A ← (1 − μ_A)A + μ_A·1[a ∈ Y]；
        a 边上的自由结构元 z ← max(0, z + ν_M(1 − A/ε_M))；z ≥ 1 时生成 ℓ，d_ℓ = 0。
    生成后 ℓ 按 C16：阈 g(d_ℓ)，通过 d_ℓ += α_ℓ（夹于 D̄_ℓ），静置退浅。"""
    st = {'d': [F(st0['d'][0]), F(st0['d'][1]), Fr(0)], 'E': 0, 's': Fr(0), 'err': [Fr(0)] * 3, 'cnt': 0, 'armed': 0,
          'A': Fr(0), 'z': Fr(0), 'gen_at': None}
    has = lambda k: m.get(k) is not None
    cap = [m['capa'], m['capb'], m['capl']]
    out = []
    for k, (P, pres, r, blk) in enumerate(trials, 1):
        P = F(P)
        live = [0, 1] + ([L] if st['E'] else [])
        prs = set(pres) | ({L} if (st['E'] and 0 in pres) else set())
        th = {x: g(m['g0'], m['cc'], st['d'][x]) for x in live}
        taus = {x: (open_step(th[x], Fr(0), P, T) if (x in prs and x not in blk) else None) for x in (0, 1, L)}
        Y, tm = _argmin(taus, live)
        full_before = st['d'][0] >= cap[0]
        reached = False
        for x in Y:
            nv = st['d'][x] + m['alpha'][x]
            if nv >= cap[x] and not (st['d'][x] >= cap[x]):
                reached = True if x == 0 else reached
            st['d'][x] = min(nv, cap[x])
        if 0 in Y and full_before:
            reached = True
        if has('sigma') and 0 in Y and full_before and not st['E']:
            st['s'] += m['alpha'][0]
            if st['s'] >= m['sigma']:
                st['E'] = 1; st['d'][L] = st['s'] - m['sigma']; st['s'] = Fr(0); st['gen_at'] = k
        if has('lamG'):
            for x in Y:
                st['err'][x] += th[x] ** 2
            if has('gng_after_full') and reached and not st['armed']:
                st['armed'] = 1; st['cnt'] = 0
            if prs and (not has('gng_after_full') or st['armed']):
                st['cnt'] += 1
                if st['cnt'] % int(m['lamG']) == 0 and not st['E']:
                    q = max((0, 1), key=lambda x: (st['err'][x], -x))
                    if q == 0 and st['err'][0] > 0:
                        st['E'] = 1; st['d'][L] = (st['d'][0] + st['d'][1]) / 2
                        st['err'][0] *= m['alphaG']; st['err'][1] *= m['alphaG']; st['err'][L] = st['err'][0]
                        st['gen_at'] = k
            st['err'] = [m['dG'] * v for v in st['err']]
        if has('hT'):                                   # GWR：a 的发放计数（习惯化）与「失配」（已满仍入）
            if 0 in Y:
                st['h'] = max(Fr(0), st.get('h', Fr(1)) + (m['alh'] * (1 - st.get('h', Fr(1))) - 1) / m['tauh'])
            if 0 in Y and full_before and not st['E'] and st.get('h', Fr(1)) < m['hT']:
                st['E'] = 1; st['d'][L] = st['d'][0] / 2; st['gen_at'] = k
        if has('nuM') and not st['E']:
            st['A'] = (1 - m['muA']) * st['A'] + m['muA'] * (1 if 0 in Y else 0)
            st['z'] = max(Fr(0), st['z'] + m['nuM'] * (1 - st['A'] / m['epsM']))
            if st['z'] >= 1:
                st['E'] = 1; st['d'][L] = Fr(0); st['gen_at'] = k
        for _ in range(r):
            st['d'] = [(1 - m['lam']) * v for v in st['d']]
            if has('sigma'):
                st['s'] = (1 - m['mus']) * st['s']
        out.append((Y, tm))
    return out, st
~~~

A.7　t16h\_ext.py　扩展库：N15 全状态与三件事记账、延迟传递对照、BIM、N14 独立实现、IZD、协议 C 与 c4 模式模型

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""《沉积动力区分式》v1.0 附录扩展库。只用标准库与 Fraction。
一、n15_run：W16-己 上 N15 的全状态实现（可给 ℓ 的初态；记出生、首次入可读池、首次首开）。
二、delayed_gate：b 的写入以 a 的状态门控（宽读法 c2；非干涉类之外的对照）。
三、bim_run：N14 的广播积分器表示 M = 1 + κc；写入读旧 M，再更新 M。
四、IZD 与 izd_run：Izhikevich 2007 模型的固定离散化（接口、采样、时间与参数全部固定）。
五、n14_run：N14 的独立实现（含共享态复位）；c4_run：a 的夹限事件切换 b 的写入律的模式模型（共享态复位协议用）。
"""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import math
import t16b_core as tb
from t16b_core import g, open_step
F = tb.F
INF = None

def _argmin(taus, keys):
    fin = [taus[k] for k in keys if taus[k] is not None]
    if not fin:
        return frozenset(), None
    t = min(fin)
    return frozenset(k for k in keys if taus[k] == t), t

# ---------------- 一、N15 全状态 ----------------
def n15_run(m, st0, T, trials):
    """st0 = {'d': (d_a, d_b, d_l), 'E': 0/1, 's': s}；trials = [(P, pres, r, block)]。返回 out, st；st['birth'], st['pool'], st['open'] 为三件事的试次号。"""
    st = {'d': [F(v) for v in st0['d']], 'E': st0.get('E', 0), 's': F(st0.get('s', 0)),
          'birth': None, 'pool': None, 'open': None}
    cap = [m['capa'], m['capb'], m['capl']]
    out = []
    for k, (P, pres, r, blk) in enumerate(trials, 1):
        P = F(P)
        live = [0, 1] + ([2] if st['E'] else [])
        prs = set(pres) | ({2} if (st['E'] and 0 in pres) else set())
        pool = [x for x in live if x in prs and x not in blk]
        if st['E'] and 2 in pool and st['pool'] is None:
            st['pool'] = k
        taus = {x: (open_step(g(m['g0'], m['cc'], st['d'][x]), Fr(0), P, T) if x in pool else None) for x in (0, 1, 2)}
        Y, tm = _argmin(taus, live)
        if 2 in Y and st['open'] is None:
            st['open'] = k
        full_before = st['d'][0] >= cap[0]
        for x in Y:
            st['d'][x] = min(st['d'][x] + m['alpha'][x], cap[x])
        if 0 in Y and full_before and not st['E']:
            st['s'] += m['alpha'][0]
            if m['sigma'] > 0 and st['s'] >= m['sigma']:
                st['E'] = 1; st['d'][2] = st['s'] - m['sigma']; st['s'] = Fr(0); st['birth'] = k
        for _ in range(r):
            st['d'] = [(1 - m['lam']) * v for v in st['d']]
            st['s'] = (1 - m['mus']) * st['s']
        out.append((Y, tm))
    return out, st

# ---------------- 二、延迟传递门控对照 ----------------
def delayed_gate(e, T=100):
    """a 用 TF3e：初态 0、容量 1/2，a 开放时 d_a ← clip(d_a + e, 0, 1/2)；b 只在 b 开放时写 (1/2)·d_a（宽读法 c2：以 a 的状态门控 b 的 K1）。
    预训练 1 次只呈现 a；测试 2 次只呈现 b，e = 0，P = 1。返回（预训练后 d_b，b 的首开步列表）。"""
    da, db = Fr(0), Fr(0)
    if open_step(g(1, 1, da), Fr(0), Fr(1), T) is not None:
        da = max(Fr(0), min(Fr(1, 2), da + e))
    pre_b = db
    out = []
    for _ in range(2):
        out.append(open_step(g(1, 1, db), Fr(0), Fr(1), T))
        db = db + Fr(1, 2) * da
    return pre_b, out

# ---------------- 三、广播积分器重参数化 ----------------
def clip(v, lo, hi):
    return lo if v < lo else (hi if v > hi else v)

def bim_run(m, d0, T, trials):
    """m: g0, cc, alpha, lam, kappa, beta, cbar, muc（与 N14 同名参数）；trials = [(P, pres, r, e)]"""
    d = [F(d0[0]), F(d0[1])]
    Mv = Fr(1)
    lo, hi = 1 - m['kappa'] * m['cbar'], 1 + m['kappa'] * m['cbar']
    out = []
    for (P, pres, r, e) in trials:
        P = F(P)
        taus = {x: (open_step(g(m['g0'], m['cc'], d[x]), Fr(0), P, T) if x in pres else None) for x in (0, 1)}
        Y, tm = _argmin(taus, (0, 1))
        for x in Y:
            d[x] = max(Fr(0), d[x] + m['alpha'] * Mv)
        if Y:
            Mv = clip(Mv + m['kappa'] * m['beta'] * e, lo, hi)
        for _ in range(r):
            Mv = 1 + (1 - m['muc']) * (Mv - 1)
            d = [(1 - m['lam']) * v for v in d]
        out.append((Y, tm))
    return out, {'d': d, 'M': Mv}

# ---------------- 四、Izhikevich 2007 的固定离散化 ----------------
IZD = dict(
    dt=Fr(1, 10),            # 每个窗口 100 ms：一个试次 = 一个窗口；一个静置拍 = 一个窗口
    rho_c=Fr(9048, 10000),   # exp(−0.1 s / τ_c)，τ_c = 1 s，取四位有理近似
    rho_d=Fr(6065, 10000),   # exp(−0.1 s / τ_d)，τ_d = 0.2 s
    s0=Fr(1, 1000),          # 强直源 0.01 μM/s × 0.1 s
    jump=Fr(1, 2),           # 奖赏使 DA 阶跃 0.5 μM
    Ap=Fr(1),                # 一次「通过」（前先后后配对）使资格迹增 A₊（归一）
    kw=Fr(10),               # 权重标度：w 每 μM·s·单位资格增 10（接口参数，固定）
    wbar=Fr(4),              # 权重上界 4（文献 0—4 mV）
)

def izd_run(m, d0, T, trials, reset_at=None):
    """深度 d_x 即权重 w_x；阈 g(w_x)。每窗口次序：①阈 ②Y ③x ∈ Y 时 E_x += A₊；有开放且 e = +1 时 D += jump（结果随反应而至；e = −1 无对应，不释放）
    ④w_x += kw·dt·E_x·D（一切 x，夹于 [0, w̄]）⑤E ← ρ_c·E，D ← ρ_d·D + s0。静置拍：只作 ④⑤。D 初值取强直稳态 s0/(1 − ρ_d)。
    reset_at：在该试次开始前把 D 复位到强直稳态（协议 C 的共享态选择性干预）。trials = [(P, pres, r, e)]"""
    P_ = IZD
    w = [F(d0[0]), F(d0[1])]
    E = [Fr(0), Fr(0)]
    Dstar = P_['s0'] / (1 - P_['rho_d'])
    D = Dstar
    out = []
    def tail():
        nonlocal D, E, w
        w = [clip(w[x] + P_['kw'] * P_['dt'] * E[x] * D, Fr(0), P_['wbar']) for x in (0, 1)]
        E = [P_['rho_c'] * v for v in E]
        D = P_['rho_d'] * D + P_['s0']
    for k, (P, pres, r, e) in enumerate(trials, 1):
        if reset_at is not None and k == reset_at:
            D = Dstar
        P = F(P)
        taus = {x: (open_step(g(m['g0'], m['cc'], w[x]), Fr(0), P, T) if x in pres else None) for x in (0, 1)}
        Y, tm = _argmin(taus, (0, 1))
        for x in Y:
            E[x] += P_['Ap']
        if Y and e == 1:
            D += P_['jump']
        tail()
        for _ in range(r):
            tail()
        out.append((Y, tm))
    return out, {'w': w, 'E': E, 'D': D}

def n14_run(m, d0, T, trials, reset_at=None):
    """N14（与 t16f_ext.e_run 同律的独立实现，加协议 C 的复位：reset_at 试次开始前 c ← 0）。trials = [(P, pres, r, e)]"""
    d = [F(d0[0]), F(d0[1])]
    c = Fr(0)
    out = []
    for k, (P, pres, r, e) in enumerate(trials, 1):
        if reset_at is not None and k == reset_at:
            c = Fr(0)
        P = F(P)
        taus = {x: (open_step(g(m['g0'], m['cc'], d[x]), Fr(0), P, T) if x in pres else None) for x in (0, 1)}
        Y, tm = _argmin(taus, (0, 1))
        for x in Y:
            d[x] = max(Fr(0), d[x] + m['alpha'] * (1 + m['kappa'] * c))
        if Y:
            c = clip(c + m['beta'] * e, -m['cbar'], m['cbar'])
        for _ in range(r):
            c = (1 - m['muc']) * c
            d = [(1 - m['lam']) * v for v in d]
        out.append((Y, tm))
    return out, {'d': d, 'c': c}

# ---------------- 五、c4 模式模型 ----------------
def c4_run(d0, T, trials, reset_at=None, cap_a=Fr(1, 2), alpha_on=Fr(1, 4)):
    """a：TF3e（通过时 d_a ← clip(d_a + e, 0, cap_a)）；b：K1 系数初为 0，a 的夹限事件（原始增量越过 cap_a）后由 c4 切为 alpha_on。
    无共用调质状态；reset_at 对它无作用（只复位共用调质，不触模式）。trials = [(P, pres, r, e)]"""
    d = [F(d0[0]), F(d0[1])]
    mode = False
    out = []
    for (P, pres, r, e) in trials:
        P = F(P)
        taus = {x: (open_step(g(1, 1, d[x]), Fr(0), P, T) if x in pres else None) for x in (0, 1)}
        Y, tm = _argmin(taus, (0, 1))
        if 0 in Y:
            raw = d[0] + e
            d[0] = max(Fr(0), min(cap_a, raw))
            if raw > cap_a:
                mode = True
        if 1 in Y and mode:
            d[1] = d[1] + alpha_on
        out.append((Y, tm))
    return out, {'d': d, 'mode': mode}
~~~

A.8　snn\_core.py　冻结 SNN 实例 s1 的程序（与《候选宿载场具化判定式》v1.0 附录 A.1 逐字相同，SHA-256 与该篇附录 B.1 所载值相同；供 A.17 的 J1 使用）

~~~python
"""冻结 SNN 实例 s1：SNN 可重放实例（程序宿载）。精确有理运算，Python 标准库，确定性，可逐事件重放。
宿载实例 χ_SNN = (P, L, B, W)：
  P 本程序；L 算法层（离散时间步）；B = {4 个输入神经元, 2 个输出 LIF 神经元, 8 个突触}；W 申报窗。
  输入神经元的发放由外部刺激序列决定（界外信号）；教学电流 teach 只在形成期注入输出 0（界外信号）。
  输出神经元：LIF，v ← v·LEAK + Σ w_ij·s_i + I_ext；v ≥ θ 时发放并复位为 0。
  可塑性：成对 STDP（迹式）：前突触迹 x_i、后突触迹 y_j 每步乘 DEC，发放时加 1；
    后神经元 j 发放时（经反传信号到达突触）w_ij += A_PLUS·x_i；前神经元 i 发放时 w_ij -= A_MINUS·y_j；
    权重夹在 [0, W_MAX]。另有可选的向基线弛豫（RELAX，默认 0）。
  干预开关：plastic（可塑性开/关）；bap（后神经元发放是否传回突触——关闭时 STDP 看不到后发放，即切断途径）。
迹应：超基线权重 z_ij = w_ij − W0。
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
import hashlib
N_IN, N_OUT = 4, 2
W0 = F(1, 4)
LEAK, THETA, DEC = F(3, 4), F(1), F(1, 2)
A_PLUS, A_MINUS, W_MAX = F(1, 8), F(1, 16), F(2)
PAT = {'A': (1, 1, 0, 0), 'B': (0, 0, 1, 1), 'AB': (1, 1, 1, 1), 'none': (0, 0, 0, 0)}

def init(w=None):
    return dict(w=[[W0 if w is None else w[i][j] for j in range(N_OUT)] for i in range(N_IN)],
                v=[F(0)] * N_OUT, x=[F(0)] * N_IN, y=[F(0)] * N_OUT)

def step(st, inp, teach=(0, 0), plastic=True, bap=True, relax=F(0)):
    """一步：返回 (新状态, 事件记录)。事件记录列出前发放、后发放与每个突触的可塑性分项（正、负、弛豫）。"""
    w = [row[:] for row in st['w']]
    s_in = PAT[inp]
    v = [st['v'][j] * LEAK + sum(w[i][j] * s_in[i] for i in range(N_IN)) + teach[j] for j in range(N_OUT)]
    s_out = [1 if v[j] >= THETA else 0 for j in range(N_OUT)]
    v = [F(0) if s_out[j] else v[j] for j in range(N_OUT)]
    x = [st['x'][i] * DEC + s_in[i] for i in range(N_IN)]
    y = [st['y'][j] * DEC + s_out[j] for j in range(N_OUT)]
    comp = {}
    for i in range(N_IN):
        for j in range(N_OUT):
            pos = A_PLUS * x[i] if (plastic and bap and s_out[j]) else F(0)
            neg = -A_MINUS * y[j] if (plastic and s_in[i] and bap) else F(0)
            rel = -relax * (w[i][j] - W0) if plastic else F(0)
            nw = min(W_MAX, max(F(0), w[i][j] + pos + neg + rel))
            comp[(i, j)] = (pos, neg, rel, nw - w[i][j])
            w[i][j] = nw
    ev = dict(inp=inp, teach=teach, s_in=s_in, s_out=tuple(s_out), comp=comp)
    return dict(w=w, v=v, x=x, y=y), ev

def run(hist, st=None, **kw):
    """hist：[(输入名, 教学电流)]。返回 (末态, 事件日志)。"""
    st = st or init()
    log = []
    for inp, teach in hist:
        st, ev = step(st, inp, teach, **kw)
        log.append(ev)
    return st, log

def log_hash(log):
    """冻结日志的指纹：事件按确定顺序序列化后取 SHA-256。"""
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    for ev in log:
        h.update(repr((ev['inp'], ev['teach'], ev['s_in'], ev['s_out'],
                       sorted((k, tuple(str(c) for c in v)) for k, v in ev['comp'].items()))).encode())
    return h.hexdigest()

def z(st):
    return [[st['w'][i][j] - W0 for j in range(N_OUT)] for i in range(N_IN)]
~~~

A.9　run16\_1.py　运行入口：节 R0—R6（命题 4-1、16-7、4-3；N2；N3；N5；签名表；乙档误判界）

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""《沉积动力区分式》v1.0 附录程序 run16_1.py（节号 R1—R6）。
运行：python3 run16_1.py。输出确定；只作有限核验、显式见证与对照；全称结论以正文的证明为准。
"""
import sys, os, hashlib, random, math
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import t16b_core as tb
from t16b_core import M, new_state, run, tr, seqstr, ystr, BOTH
R = random.Random(16101)
A, B = frozenset((0,)), frozenset((1,))
here = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))

def rq(lo, hi, den=12):
    a = math.ceil(Fr(lo) * den)
    b = math.floor(Fr(hi) * den)
    return Fr(R.randint(a, b), den)

def sha(p):
    with open(p, 'rb') as fh:
        return hashlib.sha256(fh.read()).hexdigest()

def rand_g():
    return (rq(Fr(1, 4), 1), rq(Fr(1, 4), 1)), (rq(Fr(1, 4), 2), rq(Fr(1, 4), 2))

def rand_C(kind='C', cap=True, same=False, **extra):
    if same:
        g0 = rq(Fr(1, 4), 1); c = rq(Fr(1, 4), 2)
        g0s, cs = (g0, g0), (c, c)
        a = rq(Fr(1, 12), 1); al = (a, a)
        cp = rq(Fr(1, 2), 3) if cap else None
        caps = (cp, cp)
    else:
        g0s, cs = rand_g()
        al = (rq(0, 1), rq(0, 1))
        caps = (rq(Fr(1, 2), 3), rq(Fr(1, 2), 3)) if cap else (None, None)
    lam = (rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), rq(Fr(1, 12), 1))
    return M(kind, g0=g0s, c=cs, alpha=al, lam=lam, cap=caps, **extra)

def rand_d(m, top=3):
    cp = m.get('cap')
    out = []
    for x in (0, 1):
        hi = top if (cp is None or cp[x] is None) else min(top, cp[x])
        out.append(rq(0, hi))
    return tuple(out)

def rand_trials(nmax=10, Pchoices=(Fr(1, 3), Fr(1, 2), 1, Fr(3, 2)), rest_max=3, allow_u=True, allow_pres=True):
    n = R.randint(1, nmax)
    out = []
    for _ in range(n):
        P = R.choice(Pchoices)
        u = R.randint(0, 1) if allow_u else 0
        pres = R.choice([BOTH, BOTH, BOTH, A, B]) if allow_pres else BOTH
        out.append((Fr(P), u, pres, R.randint(0, rest_max)))
    return out

print('《沉积动力区分式》v1.0 附录程序 run16_1.py')
print('=' * 72)
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ R0
print('\nR0　版本')
print('  t16b_core.py SHA-256：' + sha(os.path.join(here, 't16b_core.py')))
print('  阈族 G：g(d) = g0/(1 + c·d)；W16-戊 网格 p_t = h + t·P/T。')
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ R1
print('\nR1　C16 v0.2（K1 + K2(λ > 0) + K3 夹限 + K6）：I1、I2、∅ 吸收；对称初态的两路呈现协议；呈现集破对称')
n_tot = 0; v1 = v2 = va = 0
for _ in range(3000):
    m = rand_C('C', cap=R.random() < 0.7)
    st = new_state(m, d=rand_d(m))
    trials = rand_trials()
    out, _ = run(m, st, R.choice([3, 4, 7, 10]), trials)
    n_tot += 1
    v1 += tb.viol_I1(out, trials) is not None
    v2 += tb.viol_I2(out, trials) is not None
    va += tb.viol_absorb(out, trials) is not None
print('  随机 %d 条（含静置、呈现集、强化输入、P ∈ {1/3, 1/2, 1, 3/2}）：I1 违反 %d，I2 违反 %d，∅ 吸收违反 %d' % (n_tot, v1, v2, va))
# 对称初态：m_C 与 m_B（N1b）自对称零初态，λ = 1/10（K2 要求 λ > 0），两路都呈现的协议穷举
mC = M('C', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(1, 1), lam=(Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 10)), cap=(None, None))
mB = M('N1', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(1, 1), lam=(Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 10)), cap=(None, None), phi=Fr(1, 2), mu=1, fcap=Fr(1, 2))
import itertools
cnt = diff = 0
for n in range(1, 6):
    for rests in itertools.product((0, 1, 3), repeat=n):
        for T in (3, 4, 10):
            for P in (Fr(1, 2), 1, Fr(3, 2)):
                trials = [(Fr(P), 0, BOTH, r) for r in rests]
                o1, _ = run(mC, new_state(mC), T, trials)
                o2, _ = run(mB, new_state(mB), T, trials)
                cnt += 1
                diff += [y for y, _ in o1] != [y for y, _ in o2]
print('  对称初态（λ = 1/10，两路都呈现，n ≤ 5，静置 ∈ {0,1,3}，T ∈ {3,4,10}，P ∈ {1/2,1,3/2}）：%d 个协议，Y 序列不同者 %d' % (cnt, diff))
trials = [(Fr(1), 0, A, 0), (Fr(1), 0, BOTH, 0)]
o1, _ = run(mC, new_state(mC), 4, trials)
o2, _ = run(mB, new_state(mB), 4, trials)
print('  呈现集协议〔只呈现 a，P = 1〕→〔两路呈现，P = 1〕，T = 4：m_C：%s；m_B：%s（对称盲区在 W16-戊 的 Π₀ 内被打破）' % (seqstr(o1, True), seqstr(o2, True)))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ R2
print('\nR2　候选 N2「沁溢」')
# (a) I2、∅ 吸收
v2 = va = v1 = 0
for _ in range(3000):
    m = rand_C('N2', cap=True)
    st = new_state(m, d=rand_d(m))
    trials = rand_trials()
    out, _ = run(m, st, R.choice([3, 4, 7, 10]), trials)
    v2 += tb.viol_I2(out, trials) is not None
    va += tb.viol_absorb(out, trials) is not None
    v1 += tb.viol_I1(out, trials) is not None
print('  (a) 随机 3000 条：I2 违反 %d，∅ 吸收违反 %d（定理 N2-1：二者在 N2 上成立）；I1 违反 %d（I1 在 N2 上不成立）' % (v2, va, v1))
# (b) 显式见证
m = M('N2', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(1, 1), lam=(Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 10)), cap=(2, 2))
out, st = run(m, new_state(m, d=(1, 0)), 10, tr(6))
mc = M('C', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(1, 1), lam=(Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 10)), cap=(2, 2))
outc, stc = run(mc, new_state(mc, d=(1, 0)), 10, tr(6))
print('  (b) 见证 g = 1/(1+d)，α = 1，D̄ = 2，初态 (1, 0)，P = 1，T = 10，静置 0：N2：%s，末态 %s；C16 v0.2 同参：%s，末态 %s'
      % (seqstr(out, True), tuple(st['d']), seqstr(outc, True), tuple(stc['d'])))
# (c) 定理 N2-2：N2₌ 上的首次纳入界与终态
amin, Dmax = Fr(1, 6), Fr(3)
Kb = 1 + 2 * math.ceil(Dmax / amin)
worst = 0; badK = badF = 0; nU = 0
for _ in range(3000):
    m = rand_C('N2', cap=True, same=True)
    if m['alpha'][0] < amin or m['cap'][0] > Dmax:
        continue
    st = new_state(m, d=rand_d(m))
    T = R.choice([4, 10, 20, 50])
    out, _ = run(m, st, T, tr(Kb + 3))
    if len(out[0][0]) != 1:
        continue
    nU += 1
    x = next(iter(out[0][0])); o = 1 - x
    k1 = next((k for k, (Y, _) in enumerate(out) if o in Y), None)
    if k1 is None or k1 + 1 > Kb:
        badK += 1
    else:
        worst = max(worst, k1 + 1)
    K = 1 + 2 * math.ceil(m['cap'][0] / m['alpha'][0])
    if any(Y != BOTH for (Y, _) in out[K - 1:]):
        badF += 1
print('  (c) N2₌（两路同阈机制、同 α ≥ 1/6、同容量 ≤ 3），首试次独先的 %d 条：首次纳入另一路晚于界 1 + 2⌈D̄_max/α_min⌉ = %d 者 %d，实测最晚 %d；'
      '自 K = 1 + 2⌈D̄/α⌉ 起非 {a,b} 者 %d' % (nU, Kb, badK, worst, badF))
# (d) 定理 N2-3：容量接管
m = M('N2', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2)), lam=(Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 10)), cap=(1, 3))
out, st = run(m, new_state(m, d=(Fr(1, 2), 0)), 20, tr(14))
print('  (d) 容量接管见证（D̄_a = 1 < D̄_b = 3，α = 1/2，初态 (1/2, 0)，T = 20）：%s，末态 %s' % (seqstr(out), tuple(st['d'])))
bad = cnt = 0
for _ in range(2000):
    g0 = rq(Fr(1, 2), 1); c = rq(Fr(1, 2), 2); a = rq(Fr(1, 6), 1)
    Da = rq(Fr(1, 2), 2); Db = Da + rq(Fr(1, 2), 2)
    T = 200
    m = M('N2', g0=(g0, g0), c=(c, c), alpha=(a, a), lam=(Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 10)), cap=(Da, Db))
    gap = g0 / (1 + c * Da) - g0 / (1 + c * Db)
    if not (Fr(1, T) < gap):
        continue
    st = new_state(m, d=(rq(0, Da), rq(0, Da)))
    out, stf = run(m, st, T, tr(1 + 2 * math.ceil(Db / a) + 2 * math.ceil(Da / a) + 4))
    if len(out[0][0]) != 1:
        continue
    cnt += 1
    if out[-1][0] != B:
        bad += 1
print('  (d′) 随机 %d 条（D̄_a < D̄_b，P/T < g(D̄_a) − g(D̄_b)，首试次独先）：末试次不为 {b} 者 %d' % (cnt, bad))
# (e) 甲档（条件于可观测事件 U：首试次独先）：N2₌ 对 C16 v0.2
n = Kb
cC = cN = 0; badC = badN = 0
for _ in range(2000):
    for kind in ('C', 'N2'):
        m = rand_C(kind, cap=True, same=(kind == 'N2'))
        if kind == 'N2' and (m['alpha'][0] < amin or m['cap'][0] > Dmax):
            continue
        st = new_state(m, d=rand_d(m))
        out, _ = run(m, st, R.choice([4, 10, 20]), tr(n))
        if len(out[0][0]) != 1:
            continue
        last2 = len(out[-1][0]) == 2
        if kind == 'C':
            cC += 1; badC += last2
        else:
            cN += 1; badN += (not last2)
print('  (e) 协议 q_N2（n = %d 试次，P = 1，静置 0，两路呈现）于事件 U：C16 v0.2 %d 条中末试次两路同开 %d；N2₌ %d 条中末试次非两路同开 %d' % (n, cC, badC, cN, badN))
# (f) 与 fill-and-spill（独立实现）的共轭
same = tot = 0
for _ in range(2000):
    m = rand_C('N2', cap=True)
    d0 = rand_d(m)
    fsm = M('FS', g0=m['g0'], c=m['c'], V=m['alpha'], Cap=m['cap'], leak=m['lam'])
    trials = rand_trials(allow_u=False)
    T = R.choice([3, 4, 7, 10])
    o1, s1 = run(m, new_state(m, d=d0), T, trials)
    o2, s2 = run(fsm, tb.fs_state(S=d0), T, trials)
    tot += 1
    same += (o1 == o2 and s1['d'] == s2['S'])
print('  (f) N2 与 fill-and-spill∘首开竞争（独立实现，改名 d↔S、α↔V、D̄↔Cap、λ↔leak）：随机 %d 条，读数与末态全同 %d' % (tot, same))
# (g) 与 c4 组合 HSsat 的比较
eq_lat = tot_lat = eq_gen = tot_gen = eq_gen_read = 0
for _ in range(2000):
    m = rand_C('N2', cap=True, same=True)
    a = m['alpha'][0]; cp = m['cap'][0]
    hs = M('HSsat', g0=m['g0'], c=m['c'], alpha=m['alpha'], lam=m['lam'], cap=m['cap'])
    trials = rand_trials(allow_u=False)
    T = R.choice([4, 10])
    kmax = math.floor(cp / a)
    d_lat = (cp - R.randint(0, kmax) * a, cp - R.randint(0, kmax) * a)
    o1, s1 = run(m, new_state(m, d=d_lat), T, [(P, u, pr, 0) for (P, u, pr, r) in trials])
    o2, s2 = run(hs, new_state(hs, d=d_lat), T, [(P, u, pr, 0) for (P, u, pr, r) in trials])
    tot_lat += 1; eq_lat += (o1 == o2 and s1['d'] == s2['d'])
    d_gen = rand_d(m)
    o1, s1 = run(m, new_state(m, d=d_gen), T, [(P, u, pr, 0) for (P, u, pr, r) in trials])
    o2, s2 = run(hs, new_state(hs, d=d_gen), T, [(P, u, pr, 0) for (P, u, pr, r) in trials])
    tot_gen += 1; eq_gen += (o1 == o2 and s1['d'] == s2['d']); eq_gen_read += (o1 == o2)
print('  (g) N2 与 c4(CLIP→串扰)（HSsat，跨越步不分溢出余量）：静置 0，格点初态（D̄ − 初深为 α 的整数倍）%d 条中读数与末态全同 %d；一般初态 %d 条中全同 %d，只比读数全同 %d'
      % (tot_lat, eq_lat, tot_gen, eq_gen, eq_gen_read))
m = M('N2', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(1, 1), lam=(Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 10)), cap=(2, 2))
hs = M('HSsat', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(1, 1), lam=(Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 10)), cap=(2, 2))
o1, s1 = run(m, new_state(m, d=(Fr(3, 2), 0)), 20, tr(3))
o2, s2 = run(hs, new_state(hs, d=(Fr(3, 2), 0)), 20, tr(3))
print('      跨越步见证（初态 (3/2, 0)，T = 20）：N2 %s 末态 %s；HSsat %s 末态 %s' % (seqstr(o1, True), tuple(s1['d']), seqstr(o2, True), tuple(s2['d'])))
# (h) 双臂探针（同一初态 d = (0, 0) 的两次独立运行）：臂一〔只呈现 b〕；臂二〔只呈现 a × m，再只呈现 b〕；比较末试次 τ_b
amin2, amax2, Dmin2, Dmax2 = Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1), Fr(2)
g0min2, cmin2, cmax2 = Fr(3, 4), Fr(1, 2), Fr(2)
m_small = math.ceil(Dmin2 / amax2) - 1
dlt = Fr(1, 4)
m_large = math.ceil((Dmax2 + dlt) / amin2)
Tpr = 400
print('  (h) 双臂探针 q_I4(m)：类界 α ∈ [%s, %s]、D̄ ∈ [%s, %s]、g0 ≥ %s、c ∈ [%s, %s]；m_small = %d（m_small·α_max < D̄_min），m_large = %d，T = %d'
      % (amin2, amax2, Dmin2, Dmax2, g0min2, cmin2, cmax2, m_small, m_large, Tpr))

def arm_tau_b(m, mm):
    o1, _ = run(m, new_state(m), Tpr, [(Fr(1), 0, B, 0)])
    o2, _ = run(m, new_state(m), Tpr, [(Fr(1), 0, A, 0)] * mm + [(Fr(1), 0, B, 0)])
    return o1[-1][1], o2[-1][1]

def rand_box(kind, **extra):
    g0 = (rq(g0min2, 1), rq(g0min2, 1)); c = (rq(cmin2, cmax2), rq(cmin2, cmax2))
    al = (rq(amin2, amax2), rq(amin2, amax2)); cp = (rq(Dmin2, Dmax2), rq(Dmin2, Dmax2))
    return M(kind, g0=g0, c=c, alpha=al, lam=(rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), rq(Fr(1, 12), 1)), cap=cp, **extra)

rows = {}
for _ in range(400):
    for kind in ('C', 'N1', 'N3', 'HOM', 'TF3', 'N2', 'HS', 'STF'):
        if kind == 'N1':
            m = rand_box('N1', phi=rq(0, 1), mu=rq(0, 1))
        elif kind == 'N3':
            m = rand_box('N3', rho=rq(0, 1), muh=rq(0, 1))
        elif kind == 'HOM':
            m = rand_box('HOM', kH=rq(0, 1), Hbar=rq(0, Fr(11, 12)), nu=rq(0, 1))
        elif kind == 'HS':
            m = rand_box('HS', kappa=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1))
        elif kind == 'STF':
            m = rand_box('STF', kF=rq(Fr(1, 4), 1), Fbar=rq(Fr(1, 4), Fr(11, 12)), muF=rq(0, 1))
        else:
            m = rand_box(kind)
        r = rows.setdefault(kind, [0, 0, 0])
        t1, t2 = arm_tau_b(m, m_small)
        u1, u2 = arm_tau_b(m, m_large)
        r[0] += 1; r[1] += (t2 != t1); r[2] += (u2 != u1)
for kind in ('C', 'N1', 'N3', 'HOM', 'TF3', 'N2', 'HS', 'STF'):
    r = rows[kind]
    print('      %-4s %d 条：m_small 时 τ_b 改变 %d；m_large 时 τ_b 改变 %d' % (kind, r[0], r[1], r[2]))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ R3
print('\nR3　候选 N3「汇待合出」')
v1 = v1p = v2 = va = 0
for _ in range(3000):
    m = rand_C('N3', cap=R.random() < 0.7, rho=rq(0, 1), muh=rq(0, 1))
    st = new_state(m, d=rand_d(m))
    trials = rand_trials()
    out, _ = run(m, st, R.choice([3, 4, 7, 10]), trials)
    v1 += tb.viol_I1(out, trials) is not None
    v1p += tb.viol_I1p(out, trials) is not None
    v2 += tb.viol_I2(out, trials) is not None
    va += tb.viol_absorb(out, trials) is not None
print('  (a) 随机 3000 条（零初存量）：I1′（第 k 试次开始时 h_k = 0：首试次或前一试次非 ∅）违反 %d，I2 违反 %d（定理 N3-1：二者在 N3 上成立）；∅ 吸收违反 %d（N3 上不成立）' % (v1p, v2, va))
print('      I1（不加「前一试次非 ∅」）违反 %d：存量使网格相位移动，独先可在存量清零后变为同开（见证见下）' % v1)
m = M('N3', g0=(Fr(19, 20), 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(0, 0), lam=(Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 10)), cap=(2, 2), rho=Fr(1, 2), muh=0)
st = new_state(m); st['h'] = Fr(3, 50)
out, _ = run(m, st, 10, tr(2))
print('      见证 θ = (19/20, 1)，α = 0，初存量 h = 3/50，P = 1，T = 10：%s' % seqstr(out, True))
# (b) 首次开放的闭式：P_lo(1 + ρ + … + ρ^{j−1}) ≥ θ_min 的最小 j
bad = cnt = 0
for _ in range(2000):
    m = rand_C('N3', cap=True, rho=rq(Fr(1, 2), 1), muh=0)
    d0 = rand_d(m)
    st = new_state(m, d=d0)
    thmin = min(tb.theta(m, st, 0), tb.theta(m, st, 1))
    Plo = rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 2))
    if Plo >= thmin:
        continue
    rho = m['rho']
    jstar = None
    acc = Fr(0)
    for j in range(1, 200):
        acc += Plo * rho ** (j - 1)
        if acc >= thmin:
            jstar = j; break
    out, _ = run(m, st, 10, tr(200, P=Plo))
    first = next((k + 1 for k, (Y, _) in enumerate(out) if Y), None)
    cnt += 1
    bad += (first != jstar)
print('  (b) 首次开放试次 j* 的闭式（静置 0，P_lo < θ_min）：随机 %d 条，与运行不符 %d' % (cnt, bad))
# (c) 汇待只移起点、不改首开者（P/T < θ_b − θ_a 时）
bad = cnt = 0
for _ in range(3000):
    m = rand_C('N3', cap=True, rho=rq(0, 1), muh=rq(0, 1))
    st = new_state(m, d=rand_d(m))
    st['h'] = rq(0, 1)
    th = [tb.theta(m, st, x) for x in (0, 1)]
    if th[0] == th[1]:
        continue
    lo = 0 if th[0] < th[1] else 1
    P = rq(Fr(1, 2), 2); T = R.choice([10, 20, 50])
    if not (P / T < abs(th[1] - th[0])):
        continue
    if st['h'] >= th[lo]:
        continue
    Y, t = tb.trial(m, st, T, P)
    if Y == frozenset():
        continue
    cnt += 1
    bad += (Y != frozenset((lo,)))
print('  (c) 存量 h < θ_min、P/T < |θ_a − θ_b| 时首开者恒为阈低者：随机 %d 次，不符 %d' % (cnt, bad))
# (d) 甲档：q_N3（n 试次，P_lo，静置 0）——C16v0.2[有界]、N1、HOM、STF 对 N3[ρ ≥ ρ_min]
g0min, cmax, Dmax3 = Fr(3, 4), Fr(1), Fr(2)
thfloor = g0min / (1 + cmax * Dmax3)
Plo = Fr(1, 5)
rho_min = Fr(7, 8)
assert Plo < thfloor and Plo / (1 - rho_min) > 1
jmax = next(j for j in range(1, 1000) if Plo * (1 - rho_min ** j) / (1 - rho_min) >= 1)
n3 = jmax + 1
print('  (d) 有界类：g0 ≥ %s、c ≤ %s、D̄ ≤ %s ⇒ θ ≥ θ_floor = %s；P_lo = %s，ρ_min = %s，j*_max = %d，协议长 n = %d' % (g0min, cmax, Dmax3, thfloor, Plo, rho_min, jmax, n3))

def rand_bounded(kind, **extra):
    g0s = (rq(g0min, 1), rq(g0min, 1)); cs = (rq(Fr(1, 4), cmax), rq(Fr(1, 4), cmax))
    caps = (rq(Fr(1, 2), Dmax3), rq(Fr(1, 2), Dmax3))
    return M(kind, g0=g0s, c=cs, alpha=(rq(0, 1), rq(0, 1)), lam=(rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), rq(Fr(1, 12), 1)), cap=caps, **extra)

stats = {}
for _ in range(1500):
    for kind in ('C', 'N1', 'HOM', 'STF', 'N3'):
        if kind == 'C':
            m = rand_bounded('C')
        elif kind == 'N1':
            m = rand_bounded('N1', phi=rq(0, 1), mu=rq(0, 1))
        elif kind == 'HOM':
            m = rand_bounded('HOM', kH=rq(0, 1), Hbar=rq(0, Fr(11, 12)), nu=rq(0, 1))
        elif kind == 'STF':
            m = rand_bounded('STF', kF=rq(0, 1), Fbar=rq(0, Fr(11, 12)), muF=rq(0, 1))
        else:
            m = rand_bounded('N3', rho=rq(rho_min, 1), muh=rq(0, 1))
        st = new_state(m, d=rand_d(m, top=Dmax3))
        out, _ = run(m, st, R.choice([4, 10, 20]), tr(n3, P=Plo))
        ys = [Y for (Y, _) in out]
        allempty = all(not Y for Y in ys)
        succ_then_fail = any(ys[k] and not ys[k + 1] for k in range(len(ys) - 1))
        s = stats.setdefault(kind, [0, 0, 0])
        s[0] += 1; s[1] += allempty; s[2] += succ_then_fail
for kind in ('C', 'N1', 'HOM', 'N3', 'STF'):
    s = stats[kind]
    print('      %-4s %d 条：全 ∅ %d；出现「开放后复归 ∅」%d' % (kind, s[0], s[1], s[2]))
# (e) 与 LIF（独立实现）的共轭
same = tot = 0
for _ in range(2000):
    m = rand_C('N3', cap=True, rho=rq(0, 1), muh=rq(0, 1))
    d0 = rand_d(m); h0 = rq(0, 1)
    lif = M('LIF', g0=m['g0'], c=m['c'], alpha=m['alpha'], lam=m['lam'], cap=m['cap'], rhoV=m['rho'], muV=m['muh'])
    trials = rand_trials(allow_u=False)
    T = R.choice([3, 4, 7, 10])
    st = new_state(m, d=d0); st['h'] = h0
    o1, s1 = run(m, st, T, trials)
    o2, s2 = run(lif, tb.lif_state(V=h0, d=d0), T, trials)
    tot += 1
    same += (o1 == o2 and s1['d'] == s2['d'] and s1['h'] == s2['V'])
print('  (e) N3 与入口 LIF（独立实现，改名 h↔V、ρ↔ρ_V、μ_h↔μ_V）：随机 %d 条，读数与末态全同 %d' % (tot, same))
# (f) 快漏盲区与间隔—漏失条件
m = M('N3', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2)), lam=(Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 10)), cap=(2, 2), rho=Fr(1, 2), muh=0)
out, _ = run(m, new_state(m), 10, tr(30, P=Fr(1, 3)))
print('  (f) 快漏见证 ρ = 1/2、P_lo = 1/3、θ = 1：P_lo/(1 − ρ) = 2/3 < 1，30 试次：%s' % ('全 ∅' if all(not Y for Y, _ in out) else seqstr(out)))
m = M('N3', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2)), lam=(Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 10)), cap=(2, 2), rho=Fr(9, 10), muh=Fr(1, 2))
for r in (0, 1, 3):
    out, _ = run(m, new_state(m), 10, tr(40, P=Fr(1, 4), r=r))
    first = next((k + 1 for k, (Y, _) in enumerate(out) if Y), None)
    q = Fr(9, 10) * (1 - Fr(1, 2)) ** r
    print('      ρ = 9/10、μ_h = 1/2、P_lo = 1/4、θ = 1，试次间静置 r = %d（每间隔保留因子 q = %s，P_lo/(1 − q) = %s）：首次开放试次 %s'
          % (r, q, Fr(1, 4) / (1 - q), first))
# (g) 内稳态（时间型）对 N3（驱动型）：长静置的阈下探针
mh = M('HOM', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(0, 0), lam=(Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 10)), cap=(2, 2), kH=Fr(1, 4), Hbar=Fr(3, 4), nu=0)
mn = M('N3', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(0, 0), lam=(Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 10)), cap=(2, 2), rho=Fr(9, 10), muh=Fr(1, 2))
for mm in (mh, mn):
    o1, _ = run(mm, new_state(mm), 10, tr(12, P=Fr(1, 2), r=0))
    o2, _ = run(mm, new_state(mm), 10, tr(12, P=Fr(1, 2), r=6))
    print('  (g) %-3s 阈下驱动 P = 1/2：静置 0：%s ｜ 每次后静置 6 拍：%s' % (mm.kind, seqstr(o1), seqstr(o2)))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ R4
print('\nR4　候选 N5「承迹转深」（双臂协议：训练 → 延迟 R → 非强化测试）')

def two_arm(m, j_tr, R_short, R_long, n_test, T):
    res = []
    for Rd in (R_short, R_long):
        trials = [(Fr(1), 1, A, 0)] * (j_tr - 1) + [(Fr(1), 1, A, Rd)] + [(Fr(1), 0, BOTH, 0)] * n_test
        out, st = run(m, new_state(m), T, trials)
        res.append(out[j_tr:])
    return res

m5 = M('N5', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha_s=Fr(1, 2), sbar=2, sigma=Fr(1, 2), beta=Fr(1, 4), betaE=Fr(1, 4), mus=Fr(1, 4), muE=Fr(1, 2), muL=0)
sh, lo = two_arm(m5, 4, 2, 8, 10, 40)
print('  见证 N5（α_s = 1/2，s̄ = 2，σ* = 1/2，β = β_E = 1/4，μ_s = 1/4，μ_E = 1/2，μ_L = 0），训练 4 次，T = 40：')
print('    短臂 R = 2：%s' % seqstr(sh, True))
print('    长臂 R = 8：%s' % seqstr(lo, True))
# 自发恢复
trials = [(Fr(1), 1, A, 0)] * 3 + [(Fr(1), 1, A, 8)] + [(Fr(1), 0, BOTH, 0)] * 6 + [(Fr(1), 0, BOTH, 12)] + [(Fr(1), 0, BOTH, 0)] * 2
out, st = run(m5, new_state(m5), 400, trials)
print('    长臂消退后静置 12 拍再测（T = 400）：%s；末态 s = %s，E = %s' % (seqstr(out[4:], True), st['s'][0], st['E'][0]))
# 随机核验：各类在双臂协议上的签名
sig = {}
for _ in range(1200):
    T = 60; j_tr = 4; n_test = 12
    kinds = ['N5', 'C', 'TF3', 'RW', 'TWO']
    for kind in kinds:
        g0 = rq(Fr(3, 4), 1); c = rq(Fr(1, 2), 2)
        if kind == 'N5':
            m = M('N5', g0=(g0, g0), c=(c, c), alpha_s=rq(Fr(1, 2), 1), sbar=rq(Fr(3, 2), 3), sigma=rq(Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)),
                  beta=rq(Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)), betaE=rq(Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)), mus=rq(Fr(1, 6), Fr(1, 3)), muE=rq(Fr(1, 3), Fr(2, 3)), muL=0)
            sR_short = min(j_tr * m['alpha_s'], m['sbar']) * (1 - m['mus']) ** 1
            if sR_short < m['sigma']:
                continue
        elif kind == 'C':
            m = M('C', g0=(g0, g0), c=(c, c), alpha=(rq(Fr(1, 4), 1),) * 2, lam=(rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 3)),) * 2, cap=(None, None))
        elif kind == 'TF3':
            m = M('TF3', g0=(g0, g0), c=(c, c), alpha=(rq(Fr(1, 4), 1),) * 2, lam=(rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 3)),) * 2, cap=(None, None))
        elif kind == 'RW':
            m = M('RW', g0=(g0, g0), c=(c, c), eta=rq(Fr(1, 6), Fr(5, 6)), Vbar=rq(1, 3), lam=(rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 3)),) * 2)
        else:
            m = M('TWO', g0=(g0, g0), c=(c, c), Bf=rq(Fr(1, 6), Fr(1, 2)), Bs=rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 6)), Vbar=rq(1, 3),
                  lf=rq(Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)), ls=rq(0, Fr(1, 12)))
        sh, lo = two_arm(m, j_tr, 1, 12, n_test, T)
        test_tr = [(Fr(1), 0, BOTH, 0)] * n_test
        drop = tb.viol_tau_nondecr(sh, test_tr, 0) is not None
        lose = (len(lo[0][0]) == 1 and tb.viol_I1(lo, test_tr) is not None)
        uniq_long = len(lo[0][0]) == 1
        below = True
        if kind == 'N5':
            below = min(j_tr * m['alpha_s'], m['sbar']) * (1 - m['mus']) ** 12 < m['sigma']
        s = sig.setdefault(kind, [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0])
        s[0] += 1; s[1] += drop; s[2] += uniq_long; s[3] += lose
        if below and uniq_long:
            s[4] += 1; s[5] += lose
for kind in ('N5', 'C', 'TF3', 'RW', 'TWO'):
    s = sig[kind]
    extra = ('；其中 s_R < σ* 且首测独先 %d 条，失去独先 %d' % (s[4], s[5])) if kind == 'N5' else ''
    print('  %-3s %4d 条：短臂测试段 τ 严格下降 %4d；长臂首测独先 %4d，其中测试段失去独先 %4d%s' % (kind, s[0], s[1], s[2], s[3], extra))
# u 翻转双臂：同一初态、同一协议、只把各试次 u 取反
cnt = {}
for _ in range(300):
    for kind in ('N5', 'C', 'N1', 'N2', 'N3', 'STF', 'HOM', 'HS'):
        if kind == 'N5':
            m = M('N5', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha_s=rq(Fr(1, 4), 1), sbar=rq(1, 3), sigma=rq(Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)),
                  beta=rq(Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)), betaE=rq(Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)), mus=rq(Fr(1, 6), Fr(1, 3)), muE=rq(Fr(1, 3), Fr(2, 3)), muL=0)
        else:
            m = None
            if kind == 'C':
                m = rand_C('C')
            elif kind == 'N1':
                m = rand_C('N1', phi=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), mu=rq(0, 1))
            elif kind == 'N2':
                m = rand_C('N2')
            elif kind == 'N3':
                m = rand_C('N3', rho=rq(0, 1), muh=rq(0, 1))
            elif kind == 'STF':
                m = rand_C('STF', kF=rq(0, 1), Fbar=rq(0, Fr(11, 12)), muF=rq(0, 1))
            elif kind == 'HOM':
                m = rand_C('HOM', kH=rq(0, 1), Hbar=rq(0, Fr(11, 12)), nu=rq(0, 1))
            else:
                m = rand_C('HS', kappa=rq(0, 1))
        trials = [(Fr(1), 1, A, 0)] * 3 + [(Fr(1), 0, BOTH, 0)] * 3
        flipped = [(P, 1 - u, pr, r) for (P, u, pr, r) in trials]
        o1, _ = run(m, new_state(m), 40, trials)
        o2, _ = run(m, new_state(m), 40, flipped)
        c = cnt.setdefault(kind, [0, 0])
        c[0] += 1; c[1] += (o1 != o2)
print('  u 翻转双臂（零初态，训练 3 次只呈现 a、u = 1，再测 3 次两路呈现、u = 0；另一臂 u 全部取反）：' +
      '；'.join('%s %d/%d 读数不同' % (k, cnt[k][1], cnt[k][0]) for k in ('N5', 'C', 'N1', 'N2', 'N3', 'STF', 'HOM', 'HS')))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ R5
print('\nR5　W16-戊 上的签名表（随机协议的有限核验：违反计数 / 运行数）')

def rand_member(kind):
    if kind == 'C':
        return rand_C('C')
    if kind == 'N1':
~~~
