sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import snn_core as sn
import t16b_core as tb
import t16h_ext as th
from t16b_core import M
R = random.Random(16909)
A, B, BOTH = frozenset((0,)), frozenset((1,)), frozenset((0, 1))
here = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))

def rq(lo, hi, den=12):
    a = math.ceil(Fr(lo) * den); b = math.floor(Fr(hi) * den)
    return Fr(R.randint(a, b), den)

def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(open(p, 'rb').read()).hexdigest()

print('《沉积动力区分式》v1.0 附录程序 run16_9.py')
print('=' * 72)
print('\nJ0　版本')
for f in ('snn_core.py', 't16b_core.py', 't16h_ext.py'):
    print('  %s SHA-256：%s' % (f, sha(os.path.join(here, f))))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ J1
print('\nJ1　s1 的削弱支 Φ1b：突触后迹的当前项与残余项（DEC = %s，A₋ = %s）' % (sn.DEC, sn.A_MINUS))
h = [('A', (1, 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('A', (0, 0))]
_, ev = sn.run(h)
print('  例 J1a（零初态：A 配教学电流 (1, 0) → none → A）：各拍输出 0 的发放 %s；末拍削弱分项 MINUS00 = %s（静置 1 拍后不为 0）'
      % ([e['s_out'][0] for e in ev], ev[-1]['comp'][(0, 0)][1]))
h2 = [('A', (1, 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0))] * 6 + [('none', (0, 0))] * 8 + [('A', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('A', (0, 0))]
_, e2 = sn.run(h2)
print('  例 J1b（冻结形成前缀 (A+教学, none, none) × 6，静置 8 拍，再 A@26、none@27、A@28）：输出 0 在拍 26、27、28 的发放 %s；MINUS00@28 = %s（不为 0；拍 28 有当前后发放时含当前项 −A₋）'
      % ([e2[k]['s_out'][0] for k in (26, 27, 28)], e2[28]['comp'][(0, 0)][1]))
# 残余项上界：t0 为最后一次后发放的拍；其后 r 拍无后发放；探针拍 g_j = 0 且 s_i = 1 时 MINUS_ij = −A₋·DEC^(r+1)·y_j(t0)，|MINUS| < 2^−(r+4)
cnt = ok = cur = 0
pats = ['A', 'B', 'AB', 'none']
for _ in range(3000):
    n0 = R.randint(1, 12)
    hist = [(R.choice(pats), (R.choice((0, 1)), 0)) for _ in range(n0)]
    st, log = sn.run(hist)
    fired = [k for k, e in enumerate(log) if e['s_out'][0]]
    if not fired:
        continue
    t0 = fired[-1]
    st2, log2 = sn.run(hist[:t0 + 1])
    y_t0 = st2['y'][0]
    r = len(hist) - 1 - t0
    st3, e3 = sn.step(st, 'A', (0, 0))
    if e3['s_out'][0]:
        cur += 1
        continue
    cnt += 1
    neg = e3['comp'][(0, 0)][1]
    ok += (neg == -sn.A_MINUS * sn.DEC ** (r + 1) * y_t0 and abs(neg) < Fr(1, 2 ** (r + 4)))
print('  随机 3000 条历史后加一探针拍 A（无教学）：探针拍无后发放者 %d 条，其中「MINUS00 = −A₋·DEC^(r+1)·y₀(t₀) 且 |MINUS00| < 2^−(r+4)」%d 条；探针拍有后发放（含当前项）者 %d 条另计'
      % (cnt, ok, cur))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ J2
print('\nJ2　族甲型并联两通道：恒压头与恒总流量下的绝对通量与份额')
G0, G1 = (Fr(1), Fr(1)), (Fr(2), Fr(1))
sh = lambda G: G[1] / (G[0] + G[1])
print('  例 J2a：导通 (G_a, G_b) 由 (1, 1) 变为 (2, 1)。恒压头 ΔP = 1：Q_b %s → %s，份额 %s → %s；恒总流量 I = 2：Q_b %s → %s，份额 %s → %s'
      % (G0[1], G1[1], sh(G0), sh(G1), 2 * sh(G0), 2 * sh(G1), sh(G0), sh(G1)))
same_share = head_const = flow_drop = 0
for _ in range(2000):
    Ga, Gb, dG = rq(Fr(1, 12), 3), rq(Fr(1, 12), 3), rq(Fr(1, 12), 2)
    dP, I = rq(Fr(1, 12), 2), rq(Fr(1, 12), 2)
    s0, s1 = Gb / (Ga + Gb), Gb / (Ga + dG + Gb)
    qh0, qh1 = Gb * dP, Gb * dP
    qf0, qf1 = I * s0, I * s1
    same_share += (qh0 / (Ga * dP + qh0) == s0 and qh1 / ((Ga + dG) * dP + qh1) == s1)
    head_const += (qh0 == qh1)
    flow_drop += (qf1 < qf0)
print('  随机 2000 组（G_a 增 ΔG > 0）：两种边界条件下份额相同 %d；恒压头 Q_b 不变 %d；恒总流量 Q_b 下降 %d' % (same_share, head_const, flow_drop))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ J3
print('\nJ3　定理 16-4 的盲区：只读 Y 的同参见证与完整读数下的首格胞构造')
com = dict(g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(1, 1), lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2, cap=(None, None))
c = M('C', **com); n1 = M('N1', **com, phi=Fr(1, 100), mu=1)
oC, _ = tb.run(c, tb.new_state(c), 2, tb.tr(2, P=1))
oN, _ = tb.run(n1, tb.new_state(n1), 2, tb.tr(2, P=1))
print('  例 J3a（g = 1/(1 + d)，α = 1，φ = 1/100，n = 2，P = 1，T = 2）：Y 序列相同 %s；首开步 C16 %s，N1 %s（完整读数不同）'
      % ([Y for Y, _ in oC] == [Y for Y, _ in oN], [t for _, t in oC], [t for _, t in oN]))
ok = tot = 0
for T in (1, 2, 10, 100):
    for P in (Fr(1, 10), Fr(1), Fr(3)):
        for ntr in (1, 2, 20):
            th_ = min(Fr(1, 2), P / (2 * T)); ph = (P / T - th_) / (2 * ntr)
            cm = M('C', g0=(th_, th_), c=(1, 1), alpha=(0, 0), lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2, cap=(None, None))
            nm = M('N1', g0=(th_, th_), c=(1, 1), alpha=(0, 0), lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2, cap=(None, None), phi=ph, mu=0)
            q = [(P, 0, BOTH, k % 3) for k in range(ntr)]
            ca, _ = tb.run(cm, tb.new_state(cm), T, q); na, _ = tb.run(nm, tb.new_state(nm), T, q)
            tot += 1
            ok += (ca == na and all(Y == BOTH and t == 1 for Y, t in ca))
print('  首格胞构造（α = 0，θ* = min(1/2, P/(2T))，g0 = θ*，c = 1，零初态，μ = 0，φ = (P/T − θ*)/(2n)）：T ∈ {1, 2, 10, 100}、P ∈ {1/10, 1, 3}、n ∈ {1, 2, 20}、静置按 k mod 3：%d 组中两侧完整读数全同且恒为 ({a,b}, 1) 者 %d' % (tot, ok))
ok = tot = 0
for _ in range(1000):
    T = R.choice((1, 3, 7, 40, 400)); P = rq(Fr(1, 12), 3); ntr = R.randint(1, 30)
    th_ = min(Fr(1, 2), P / (2 * T)); ph = (P / T - th_) / (2 * ntr)
    lam = rq(Fr(1, 12), 1)
    cm = M('C', g0=(th_, th_), c=(1, 1), alpha=(0, 0), lam=(lam, lam), cap=(None, None))
    nm = M('N1', g0=(th_, th_), c=(1, 1), alpha=(0, 0), lam=(lam, lam), cap=(None, None), phi=ph, mu=0)
    q = [(P, 0, BOTH, R.randint(0, 5)) for _ in range(ntr)]
    ca, _ = tb.run(cm, tb.new_state(cm), T, q); na, _ = tb.run(nm, tb.new_state(nm), T, q)
    tot += 1
    ok += (ca == na and all(Y == BOTH and t == 1 for Y, t in ca))
print('  随机 1000 个有限 Q₀ 协议（T、P、n、λ、各试次静置随机）：完整读数全同且恒为 ({a,b}, 1) 者 %d/%d' % (ok, tot))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ J4
print('\nJ4　N3 的 I1′：首试次零存量与非零存量两支')
m = M('N3', g0=(Fr(19, 20), 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(0, 0), lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2, cap=(None, None), rho=Fr(1, 2), muh=0)
q = tb.tr(2, P=1)
out, _ = tb.run(m, tb.new_state(m, h=Fr(3, 50)), 10, q)
print('  例 J4a（θ = (19/20, 1)，α = 0，h₀ = 3/50，P = 1，T = 10）：Y 序列 %s；首对 h₀ ≠ 0，不在 I1′ 的申报域内：按 h₀ 检查返回 %s；若误按 h₀ = 0 检查则在第 %s 对报违反'
      % (tb.seqstr(out), tb.viol_I1p(out, q, h0=Fr(3, 50)), tb.viol_I1p(out, q)))
out0, _ = tb.run(m, tb.new_state(m), 10, q)
print('  同参零初存量：Y 序列 %s；按 h₀ = 0 检查返回 %s' % (tb.seqstr(out0), tb.viol_I1p(out0, q)))
v0 = v1 = first_fail = n1c = 0
for _ in range(3000):
    g0 = (rq(Fr(1, 2), 1), rq(Fr(1, 2), 1))
    mm = M('N3', g0=g0, c=(rq(Fr(1, 2), 2), rq(Fr(1, 2), 2)), alpha=(rq(0, 1), rq(0, 1)), lam=(rq(Fr(1, 12), 1),) * 2,
           cap=(None, None), rho=rq(0, 1), muh=rq(0, 1))
    trials = [(Fr(R.choice((1, 1, Fr(3, 2)))), 0, BOTH, R.choice((0, 0, 0, 1))) for _ in range(R.randint(2, 8))]
    T = R.choice((3, 4, 7, 10))
    o0, _ = tb.run(mm, tb.new_state(mm), T, trials)
    v0 += tb.viol_I1p(o0, trials) is not None
    hh = rq(Fr(1, 12), min(g0) - Fr(1, 12)) if min(g0) > Fr(1, 6) else Fr(0)
    if hh > 0:
        n1c += 1
        o1, _ = tb.run(mm, tb.new_state(mm, h=hh), T, trials)
        v1 += tb.viol_I1p(o1, trials, h0=hh) is not None
        first_fail += tb.viol_I1p(o1, trials) == 0
print('  随机 3000 条，零初存量：I1′ 违反 %d；其中 %d 条另取非零初存量 h₀ ∈ (0, min θ)：申报域内（首对除外、前一试次非 ∅ 的对）违反 %d，首对 I1 失败 %d'
      % (v0, n1c, v1, first_fail))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ J5
print('\nJ5　N15 的生成计数与出生合法域')
m5 = M('N15', g0=1, cc=1, capa=1, capb=2, capl=2, alpha=(Fr(1, 10), 0, 0), lam=Fr(1, 10), sigma=1, mus=0)
o, s = th.n15_run(m5, {'d': (1, 0, 0), 'E': 0, 's': Fr(9, 10)}, 10, [(1, A, 0, ())])
print('  例 J5a（初生成量 s₀ = 9/10，σ = 1，α_a = 1/10，μ_s = 0，a 初已满）：出生于第 %s 试次；零初生成量的计数 ⌈σ/α_a⌉ = %d 不适用，非零分支的计数 ⌈(σ − s₀)/α_a⌉ = %d'
      % (s['birth'], math.ceil(m5['sigma'] / m5['alpha'][0]), math.ceil((m5['sigma'] - Fr(9, 10)) / m5['alpha'][0])))
m6 = M('N15', g0=1, cc=1, capa=1, capb=2, capl=1, alpha=(100, 0, 0), lam=Fr(1, 10), sigma=1, mus=0)
o, s = th.n15_run(m6, {'d': (1, 0, 0), 'E': 0, 's': 0}, 10, [(1, A, 0, ())])
print('  例 J5b（域外：s₀ = 0，α_a = 100 > D̄_ℓ = 1，σ = 1）：出生深度 d_ℓ = %s > D̄_ℓ = %s（违反 D̄_ℓ ≥ α_a 的前件，出生状态不合法）' % (s['d'][2], m6['capl']))
legal = lower = bound = 0
for _ in range(2000):
    al = rq(Fr(1, 12), 1); capl = R.choice((al, al + rq(0, 2), None))
    sig = rq(Fr(1, 12), 3); mus = R.choice((0, rq(0, 1)))
    s0 = R.choice((Fr(0), rq(0, sig - Fr(1, 12)) if sig > Fr(1, 12) else Fr(0)))
    mm = M('N15', g0=1, cc=1, capa=1, capb=2, capl=capl if capl is not None else 10 ** 9, alpha=(al, 0, 0), lam=Fr(1, 10), sigma=sig, mus=mus)
    rests = R.choice((0, 1))
    tr_ = [(1, A, rests if R.random() < 0.3 else 0, ()) for _ in range(80)]
    o, s = th.n15_run(mm, {'d': (1, 0, 0), 'E': 0, 's': s0}, 10, tr_)
    if s['birth'] is None:
        continue
    legal += 1
    bound += (0 <= s['d'][2] < al)
    need = math.ceil((sig - s0) / al)
    lower += s['birth'] >= need
print('  合法域随机 2000 条（σ > 0，μ_s ∈ [0, 1]，D̄_a = 1，D̄_ℓ ≥ α_a 或 ∞，0 ≤ s₀ < σ，a 初已满、只呈现 a）：出生者 %d；出生深度在 [0, α_a) 内 %d；出生试次 ≥ ⌈(σ − s₀)/α_a⌉ 者 %d'
      % (legal, bound, lower))
ex_tot = ex_ok = 0
for _ in range(1000):
    al = rq(Fr(1, 12), 1); sig = rq(Fr(1, 12), 3); s0 = R.choice((Fr(0), rq(0, sig - Fr(1, 12)) if sig > Fr(1, 12) else Fr(0)))
    mm = M('N15', g0=1, cc=1, capa=1, capb=2, capl=al, alpha=(al, 0, 0), lam=Fr(1, 10), sigma=sig, mus=0)
    o, s = th.n15_run(mm, {'d': (1, 0, 0), 'E': 0, 's': s0}, 10, [(1, A, 0, ())] * 80)
    ex_tot += 1
    ex_ok += (s['birth'] == math.ceil((sig - s0) / al))
print('  μ_s = 0、无静置的随机 1000 条：出生试次恰为 ⌈(σ − s₀)/α_a⌉ 者 %d/%d' % (ex_ok, ex_tot))
rows = []
for al in (Fr(1, 4), Fr(3, 10), Fr(1, 3), Fr(3, 8), Fr(1, 2)):
    mm = M('N15', g0=1, cc=1, capa=1, capb=2, capl=1, alpha=(al, 0, 0), lam=Fr(1, 10), sigma=1, mus=0)
    o, s = th.n15_run(mm, {'d': (1, 0, 0), 'E': 0, 's': 0}, 10, [(1, A, 0, ())] * 10)
    rows.append('α_a = %s：第 %s 试次' % (al, s['birth']))
print('  阶梯依赖（σ = 1，s₀ = 0）：' + '；'.join(rows) + '（⌈1/α_a⌉ 相同的 α_a 给出同一生成试次，平台不构成反例）')
print('\n完。')

R6　乙档：读数通道 R\_ε 下的两类误判上界（精确有理运算后转十进制）  
&nbsp;&nbsp;统计量：在申报的尾段 n′ 个试次中计报告为指定形态的次数 S；判则 S ≥ τ。一侧真值从不呈该形态，另一侧尾段恒呈该形态。  
&nbsp;&nbsp;误判上界 α ≤ P(Bin(n′, ε) ≥ τ)，β ≤ P(Bin(n′, 1 − ε) \< τ)；τ 取使二者较大者最小的值。  
&nbsp;&nbsp;ε = 1/20  n′ = 10  τ = 5   α ≤ 6.369e-05  β ≤ 2.755e-06  
&nbsp;&nbsp;ε = 1/20  n′ = 20  τ = 10  α ≤ 1.134e-08  β ≤ 5.380e-10  
&nbsp;&nbsp;ε = 1/20  n′ = 40  τ = 20  α ≤ 4.960e-16  β ≤ 2.474e-17  
&nbsp;&nbsp;ε = 1/10  n′ = 10  τ = 5   α ≤ 1.635e-03  β ≤ 1.469e-04  
&nbsp;&nbsp;ε = 1/10  n′ = 20  τ = 10  α ≤ 7.151e-06  β ≤ 7.089e-07  
&nbsp;&nbsp;ε = 1/10  n′ = 40  τ = 20  α ≤ 1.872e-10  β ≤ 1.960e-11  
&nbsp;&nbsp;ε = 1/5   n′ = 10  τ = 5   α ≤ 3.279e-02  β ≤ 6.369e-03  
&nbsp;&nbsp;ε = 1/5   n′ = 20  τ = 10  α ≤ 2.595e-03  β ≤ 5.634e-04  
&nbsp;&nbsp;ε = 1/5   n′ = 40  τ = 20  α ≤ 2.169e-05  β ≤ 5.027e-06  
&nbsp;&nbsp;N3 对有界 C16 v0.2（协议 q\_N3，j\*\_max = 8，n = 8m，统计量 S = 报告非 ∅ 的试次数）：α ≤ P(Bin(n, ε) ≥ τ)，β ≤ P(Bin(m, 1 − ε) \< τ)  
&nbsp;&nbsp;ε = 1/20  m = 10  n = 80   τ = 8   α ≤ 4.659e-02  β ≤ 1.150e-02  
&nbsp;&nbsp;ε = 1/20  m = 20  n = 160  τ = 16  α ≤ 6.707e-03  β ≤ 2.574e-03  
&nbsp;&nbsp;ε = 1/20  m = 40  n = 320  τ = 32  α ≤ 1.810e-04  β ≤ 1.296e-04  
&nbsp;&nbsp;ε = 1/40  m = 10  n = 80   τ = 8   α ≤ 8.930e-04  β ≤ 1.643e-03  
&nbsp;&nbsp;ε = 1/40  m = 20  n = 160  τ = 15  α ≤ 1.376e-05  β ≤ 6.994e-06  
&nbsp;&nbsp;ε = 1/40  m = 40  n = 320  τ = 30  α ≤ 9.742e-10  β ≤ 2.818e-09  
&nbsp;&nbsp;用法：N2 对 C16 v0.2（事件 U 取类限定「首试次独先」），形态 = 两路同开，尾段 = 第 K 至 n 试次；N3 对 C16、N1、HOM（有界类），见上表（N3 的开放不连续，故按「每 j\*\_max 试次至少一次」计）。  
完。

Q3　深度正恢复与可分辨的独先恢复分开判定：同胞例  
&nbsp;&nbsp;同胞例 s = 1/400、E = 1/200、μ\_s = 1/2、μ\_E = 3/4、T = 40：R = 1..79 中深度为正 78 次，独先 0 次；最大恢复深度 1/3200  
&nbsp;&nbsp;解析界：一切 R 有 δ\_R ≤ s·(1 − μ\_s)^R ≤ 1/800 \< δ\_need = 1/39（P = 1、T = 40、g = 1/(1 + d) 下独先所需深度的充分下界之反面：g(0) − g(δ) ≤ 1/40 ⇔ δ ≤ 1/39），故永无可分辨恢复。  
&nbsp;&nbsp;正例（run16\_1 R4 节的见证成员，消退后 s、E 为实际末态，T = 400）：R=4 深度 0.00000 裕度 g(0)−g(δ)−P/T=-0.00250 读数 {a,b}；R=8 深度 0.01419 裕度 g(0)−g(δ)−P/T=0.01149 读数 {a}；R=12 深度 0.00598 裕度 g(0)−g(δ)−P/T=0.00344 读数 {a}；R=16 深度 0.00198 裕度 g(0)−g(δ)−P/T=-0.00052 读数 {a,b}；R=24 深度 0.00020 裕度 g(0)−g(δ)−P/T=-0.00230 读数 {a,b}  
&nbsp;&nbsp;读法：可分辨独先恢复只在裕度 \> 0 的具体 R 上签（充分条件）；裕度 ≤ 0 时读数可为同开，即使深度为正。

&nbsp;&nbsp;GNG          q15a 臂甲（a 已满，只呈现 a 8 次）阻断 a 测：{ℓ}（生成于第 4 试次）；臂乙（只呈现 b 8 次）：∅（None）；q15b（a 远未满，只呈现 a 8 次）：{ℓ}  
&nbsp;&nbsp;c4(GNG@到底)   q15a 臂甲（a 已满，只呈现 a 8 次）阻断 a 测：{ℓ}（生成于第 4 试次）；臂乙（只呈现 b 8 次）：∅（None）；q15b（a 远未满，只呈现 a 8 次）：∅  
&nbsp;&nbsp;MSP          q15a 臂甲（a 已满，只呈现 a 8 次）阻断 a 测：∅（生成于第 None 试次）；臂乙（只呈现 b 8 次）：{ℓ}（4）；q15b（a 远未满，只呈现 a 8 次）：∅  
&nbsp;&nbsp;GWR          q15a 臂甲（a 已满，只呈现 a 8 次）阻断 a 测：{ℓ}（生成于第 3 试次）；臂乙（只呈现 b 8 次）：∅（None）；q15b（a 远未满，只呈现 a 8 次）：∅  
&nbsp;&nbsp;C16（无生成）     q15a 臂甲（a 已满，只呈现 a 8 次）阻断 a 测：∅（生成于第 None 试次）；臂乙（只呈现 b 8 次）：∅（None）；q15b（a 远未满，只呈现 a 8 次）：∅  
&nbsp;&nbsp;S15 随机各 300 条（n = 40）：N15 300，GNG 0，MSP 0，c4(GNG@到底) 300，GWR 300，C16 0  
&nbsp;&nbsp;生成时刻对 α\_a 的依赖（a 初态已满，只呈现 a；记生成所在试次；命题 N15-5）：  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;N15          α\_a = 1/4：第 3 试次；α\_a = 1/8：第 6 试次  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;c4(GNG@到底)   α\_a = 1/4：第 4 试次；α\_a = 1/8：第 4 试次  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;GWR          α\_a = 1/4：第 3 试次；α\_a = 1/8：第 3 试次  
完。
